Creatives RFC: obligation status

F3, M2, M3, C2, L2 and L4 are re-read from the lanes' claims and the merged code. The "Graded at" column is the last box run, innet_301026_1301, which ran 813ee8dff on a clean tree, as RFC 002 Part 11 reads it. No box run exists on the wave 11 tree: every scenario a wave-11 lane added or changed has been collected on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest and none has run, and since wave 11 changed format_satisfaction.py, format_resolver.py and the reference-format capture, every obligation needs that run to confirm its grade.

Only S5 and C2 are re-read for wave 9, from the lanes' claims and the merged code. Every other obligation keeps its wave 8 grade. No box run exists on this tree. Each scenario a wave-9 lane added or changed has been collected and none has run.

The wave 8 grade: re-graded after wave 8 against AdCP tag v3.2.7 on tree ef4d8f5fa (integrate/wave1, tree object 21e20c5a3), the merge of the six wave-8 lanes onto 2e8a147cc. Statuses and residuals are read from the lanes' claims and from the merged code. No box run exists on this tree yet: every scenario a wave-8 lane added or changed has been collected on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest and none has run, so the graded column reads "pending box run" for those obligations, and the confirming run comes later. Obligations wave 8 did not touch carry their 2026-10-30 grade, which also needs that run, since wave 8 changed format_satisfaction.py and format_resolver.py, which most of their scenarios run through.

The previous grade: re-graded 2026-10-30 against AdCP tag v3.2.7 (the version the installed SDK, adcp 9.3.0, reports) on tree c20a4c2c1 (integrate/wave1). Per-transport outcomes are read per nodeid from box run innet_291026_2041, which ran commit 59449acee, the parent of c20a4c2c1; the one commit between them changes the creative-agent stack's env recipe, run_all_tests_host.sh and an integration test, and no graded file. The 2026-10-29 grade (tree 1b5c6880b, run innet_291026_0257) and the 2026-10-28 grade (a grader and an independent skeptic, tree 5bf5677fb) are kept under each obligation. Every row links the bead that carries the full evidence and gaps.

Wave 11 merge

Only lane/w11-m3-params conflicted (format_satisfaction.py, errors/codes.py, exceptions.py).
  • One rule, one implementation: lane/w11-m3-legacy mapped a manifest shortfall to its error code through a free function, and lane/w11-m3-params through ManifestShortfall.code. The merge keeps the property with all three specific codes (size, click URL, closed value) and deletes the function.
  • No contradiction found: no feature states two outcomes for one input. The new CREATIVE_MISSING_CLICK_URL code changes no existing refusal row, because no row leaves out only a click destination. The formats the legacy rows grade declare the same required assets in the old and new capture.
  • One Alembic revision (7a74ba340f11, lane/w11-f3-fail-closed), so a single head.

The status changed since 2026-10-29 on 7: S1 (PARTIAL to IMPLEMENTED), S3 (PARTIAL to IMPLEMENTED), M6 (ABSENT to IMPLEMENTED), C2 (IMPLEMENTED to PARTIAL), E1 (IMPLEMENTED to PARTIAL), L2 (PARTIAL to IMPLEMENTED), V1 (NO LONGER REQUIRED to N/A). Failing in innet_291026_2041: the two sync-coverage refusals of F3 on a2a, mcp and rest, and the two inline-creative replace scenarios of C2 on all four transports.

What "graded" means here

  • format_kind is the canonical identifier. format_id is a retained legacy assertion: accepted, recorded and echoed, never what business logic keys on.
  • yes: every clause of the obligation that the seller implements is asserted by a BDD scenario whose steps read values off the wire, and every nodeid of that scenario passed on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest in innet_291026_2041. partly: some clauses are graded that way, and the gaps name the clauses that are not, whether unbuilt or built with no scenario. no: nothing grades the obligation.
  • comply_test_controller is not part of this seller. At the pin the canonical_formats storyboard declares requires: [controller], so it grades nothing here.
  • C1 and C3 are not applicable, and C4, G1 and G2 follow from the same posture: the owner decided on 2026-10-29 that this seller does not claim the creative protocol, and the pinned capabilities response carries the creative block only when creative is in supported_protocols.
  • A count in parentheses after a scenario is its number of collected nodeids per transport, so an outline's count is its number of rows. The current grade reads innet_291026_2041: in-process BDD (a2a, mcp, rest) and in-network BDD (e2e_rest). The outcome columns in the kept 2026-10-29 tables are from innet_291026_0257.
  • Each obligation lists the action its grade recommends for each bead. The beads themselves are not changed by this page.

Detail

S1 IMPLEMENTED Move each product's creative contract into format_options[]

2026-10-28: PARTIAL (grader PARTIAL, skeptic PARTIAL). 2026-10-29: PARTIAL. 2026-10-30: IMPLEMENTED. Wave 8 (tree ef4d8f5fa): IMPLEMENTED. Graded on all four transports: partly. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.1.

"## Seller migration\n\n1. Move each product's creative contract into `format_options[]`." (canonical-formats-migration.mdx:24). Reinforced normatively by core/product.json properties.format_options.description: "New 3.2 products MUST carry format_options; a seller MAY additionally project the same declarations to deprecated format_ids for older 3.x peers." and properties.format_ids.description: "Deprecated in AdCP 3.2; removed in AdCP 4.0. ... Products MUST carry `format_ids`, `format_options`, or both during the 3.x migration window. New products MUST author canonical `format_options[]` ... Do not author a new product from `format_ids` alone."

docs/creative/canonical-formats-migration.mdx § Seller migration (step 1, line 24) at v3.2.7; static/schemas/source/core/product.json#/properties/format_options/description and #/properties/format_ids/description at v3.2.7. Since the previous audit: unchanged in wording at the migration-guide step. The schema around it is explicit at v3.2.7: "New 3.2 products MUST carry format_options", format_ids "Deprecated in AdCP 3.2; removed in AdCP 4.0", and the root anyOf marks the "Named-format product" arm `deprecated: true`. The migration guide also says deprecated fields "should be projections derived from canonical data", never an alternative authoring model.

Wave 8, graded at ef4d8f5fa

Graded: lane w8-rfc-grading added @T-UC-GET-PRODUCTS-format-options-operator-authored (b371932bf, Given rebuilt on minimal_product_create_form and seed_property_tag_selection in 9ff061ee9). The Given posts the admin product create form on the scenario's own transport and asserts the redirect; the Thens read format_kind, params and the absence of format_ids off the wire. Collected on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest; not yet run. The read-side scenarios graded in innet_291026_2041 are unchanged in this wave. At the merge, the operator Given now records the params it posted, because E1 made the shared Then compare against the stored params.

Residual: (1) a box run must show the new scenario passing on all four transports; (2) inventory-profile authoring and the other authoring paths (sample data, mock adapter, setup scripts) have no BDD scenario; (3) rows the data migration leaves on the legacy arm are named only in its log.

Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.1 keep open until the box run.

The 2026-10-30 grade (tree c20a4c2c1, outcomes from innet_291026_2041)

Graded

Partly. Read side passes on a2a/mcp/rest/e2e_rest in innet_291026_2041 (commit 59449acee; the only commit between it and c20a4c2c1 is a test-env recipe chore): T-UC-GET-PRODUCTS-format-options-canonical-only, -v1-ref, -ref-projected, -profile-canonical, -profile-linked, T-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION-approval-unsnapshotted-canonical-only and -approval-unsnapshotted-profile-linked. All their Thens read format_kind, params and the presence or absence of format_ids off the wire, so none is vacuous. The authoring half has no BDD grading: the admin forms are covered only by tests/admin/test_product_format_options_authoring.py, and the data migration only by the unit test of its row plan.

Evidence

Every authoring path now writes the canonical arm. Admin create and edit: src/admin/blueprints/products.py:873-878, plus :1527, which clears format_ids. Inventory profiles: src/admin/blueprints/inventory_profiles.py:437, and InventoryProfile.format_options at src/core/database/models.py:2163 with format_ids now nullable at :2169. Seeds: src/core/database/database.py:258/288/323, scripts/setup/seed_products.py:68, scripts/setup/init_database.py:171/189/308, scripts/setup/storyboard_signing.py:494 (e08fd8f81). Existing rows are moved by the data migration alembic/versions/ddb20e636cee_move_legacy_format_ids_into_format_options.py (plan_canonical_moves :92, upgrade :169; 54dfaffe7 + 6b3ebf085). It projects through adcp.canonical_formats.project_legacy_format_id, fails closed per row and moves profiles first. Approval reads Product.format_contract (src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:1563, f476ac1fa). Pin: canonical-formats-migration.mdx § Seller migration step 1 at v3.2.7; core/product.json#/properties/format_ids at v3.2.7 says "Do not author a new product from format_ids alone".

Gaps

(1) No BDD scenario grades the authoring half: that an operator-created product or profile carries its contract in format_options. The admin_integration and e2e_admin BDD transports exist (T-ADMIN-ACCT-*), but no format-authoring scenario runs on them. (2) The migration leaves a row on its legacy arm when any of its ids cannot be projected (for example a custom agent's format), and also leaves a product linked to a legacy-only profile. Both are named only in the migration log. That is correct fail-closed behaviour under the registry's order, but no admin surface lists these rows. (3) Every gap the previous page listed is closed at c20a4c2c1: CREATE_SAMPLE_DATA, the mock adapter route, profiles without a canonical arm, the setup scripts, storyboard_signing.py and the missing data migration. The non-GAM adapter channel is not an S1 gap either: mock, Kevel, Triton and Broadstreet read neither format_ids nor formats_to_provide, and only GAM's orders.py:86/570 reads the declarations.

Beads

  • salesagent-ol033.20.1 (update notes): Production now meets the obligation: every authoring path is canonical and the data migration ddb20e636cee exists. Every production gap in the bead description is closed. Keep it open only for the remaining grading item, an admin-BDD scenario for canonical authoring, or close it if the owner accepts tests/admin for that half. Update the title from [PARTIAL] to [IMPLEMENTED].
  • salesagent-ol033.20.28 (update notes): Item 2 (storyboard_signing) is fixed in e08fd8f81, item 3 (GAM sizing reads format_contract, gam_inventory_service.py:1262 and dynamic_pricing_service.py:105) and item 4 (admin create against a canonical profile, 986c08ee5) are done, item 1 was dropped by the owner and item 5 was decided by the owner. Only item 6 (SDK SHOULD on params narrowing) and item 7 (docs) remain.
The 2026-10-29 grade (tree 1b5c6880b, outcomes from innet_291026_0257)

The contract has moved into format_options[] on every authoring path the seller ships, except one setup script. The admin product and inventory-profile forms author canonical declarations only, authoring declarations on an existing row retires its stored format_ids, and the CREATE_SAMPLE_DATA seed, seed_products.py and init_database.py author declarations that assert a v1_format_ref. Inventory profiles now carry their own format_options column. The read side is graded on all four transports. The authoring side is not graded by BDD, existing legacy-only product rows have no data migration, and the non-GAM adapters still receive the deprecated projection as their only format channel.

Code changes since 2026-10-28

  • e90f60fad feat(admin): author products and inventory profiles canonically (admin forms, CREATE_SAMPLE_DATA, seed_products.py, init_database.py, mock adapter route)
  • 5c258f183 feat(db): inventory profiles carry a canonical format_options arm (migration a34a2ff137da)
  • a60a26c91 fix(products): derive a canonical product's format_ids from its declarations (Product.format_contract)

The obligation still holds at v3.2.7 with the same wording (canonical-formats-migration.mdx § Seller migration step 1). core/product.json makes it normative: "New 3.2 products MUST carry format_options" and "Do not author a new product from format_ids alone".   • Production gap: existing legacy-only products are never moved. Migration b41aded6b531 only adds the column, and edit_product leaves format_ids as stored (src/admin/blueprints/products.py:1531-1539). Each legacy product keeps its contract in the deprecated arm until an operator re-authors it. Nothing lists or flags unmigrated products.
  • Production gap (scripts): scripts/setup/seed_products.py:48 and scripts/setup/init_database.py:170/191/319 seed products with format_ids only. No setup script authors format_options.
  • Scenario gap: no BDD scenario grades the authoring half of S1 (an operator-created product carries its contract in format_options). Every graded scenario seeds rows through ProductFactory. The admin authoring path is covered only by tests/admin/test_product_format_options_authoring.py, which is not BDD and does not count as graded.

  • Text gap: the feature header comment (BR-UC-GET-PRODUCTS-format-options.feature:28-30) says the divergent shape is "graded by the canonical_formats storyboard's get_seeded_divergent_product step". That storyboard requires comply_test_controller, which this seller does not include, so it grades nothing here. The claim should be removed or reworded (this affects the divergence obligation more than S1).
  • Execution path still keys on the deprecated arm for non-GAM adapters. In create_media_buy, when the request carries no format_ids, the adapter's format list is rebuilt from pkg_product.format_ids, which is the schema Product's served legacy arm (src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:3815-3838). For a canonical-only product that list is []. The approval path uses `asserted or ([] if recorded else product.format_ids or [])` (media_buy_create.py:1436), and its own comment (1417-1427) says that for mock, Kevel, Triton and Broadstreet 'the legacy arm is still the only channel a dimension has'. So for every adapter except GAM, the contract the ad server executes is the deprecated projection. A canonical-only declaration with no v1_format_ref reaches those adapters with no format dimensions. This may belong to the adapter-snapshot obligation rather than S1, but it means the contract has not fully moved into format_options.
  • The legacy-buyer validation in create_media_buy compares request format_ids only against pkg_product.format_ids (media_buy_create.py:3735-3769). A canonical-only product therefore answers accepted_values=[] to a 3.x buyer that names a format_id the declaration's v1_format_ref asserts. This is defensible as legacy behaviour, but it is another place where format_id is the key and format_options is not consulted.

Code

  • src/core/database/models.py:423 products.format_ids is nullable (Mapped[... | None], nullable=True). Migration 08cd415b3d91 dropped the NOT NULL, so a canonical-only row can be stored.
  • src/core/database/models.py:433 products.format_options is a JSONType column that holds the stored ProductFormatDeclaration documents.
  • src/core/database/models.py:561 effective_format_ids returns the product's own nullable format_ids when no inventory profile is linked. The value is not coerced to [].
  • src/core/product_conversion.py:474 format_ids is put on the wire only when the legacy arm has entries. A canonical-only product omits the key instead of sending an empty array.
  • src/core/product_conversion.py:488 format_options is carried onto the Product when the row has it, independently of format_ids.
  • src/core/product_conversion.py:497 The anyOf check refuses a product only when BOTH arms are missing. The old check that refused on missing format_ids alone (the audit's 408-414 evidence) is gone.
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:723 announced_legacy_format_arm never projects v1_format_ref into format_ids, so a canonical-only product announces no legacy arm.
  • src/core/tools/products.py:587 The filters.format_kinds filter is keyed on product.format_options, so canonical-only products can be found through the canonical filters.
  • src/core/tools/products.py:632 The standard_formats_only filter reads the canonical kinds from format_options, so a canonical-only product no longer passes this filter vacuously.
  • src/admin/blueprints/products.py:883 The admin create path parses authored format_options against the pinned type and refuses only when there is no format arm at all (line 887). A canonical-only product can be authored here.

Gaps

  • The baseline run innet_181026_1634 (commit 578c00795) ran on SDK 9.0.0b3, which reports spec 3.2.3, not v3.2.7. Between that commit and HEAD the feature file changed only in its @source comments, and the step file, product_conversion.py, format_resolver.py and models.py did not change. The pin text is also unchanged between v3.2.3 and v3.2.7. So the result should carry over, but it has not been re-run on the v3.2.7 SDK (adcp==9.3.0).
  • The audit's evidence is out of date: the ValueError on empty format_ids at src/core/product_conversion.py:408-414 no longer exists. The only remaining refusal is the neither-arm case at src/core/product_conversion.py:497.
  • Residual production gap, related to S2 but outside its 'servable' wording: a product linked to an inventory profile always serves the profile's format_ids. The profile's format_ids column is NOT NULL (src/core/database/models.py:2120), effective_format_ids prefers it (src/core/database/models.py:561), and InventoryProfile has no format_options column. So a profile-linked product cannot be canonical-only. This is tracked as salesagent-8qela.154 and noted in docs/rfcs/002-creative-format-model.md:694.
  • The canonical_formats storyboard step get_seeded_v2_only_product grades nothing here, because it needs comply_test_controller and this seller has none. Only the BDD scenarios above grade S2.
  • A buy path still keys on the legacy arm for a canonical-only product. In create_media_buy's package loop, src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:3735 checks `if matching_package and matching_package.format_ids:`, and :3749 then compares the buyer's legacy format_ids against `product_format_identities(pkg_product.format_ids)`. For a canonical-only product that set is empty, so every requested id lands in `unsupported_formats` and AdCPValidationError (VALIDATION_ERROR, accepted_values=[]) is raised. This happens even though _resolve_package_selectors / resolve_routes (format_satisfaction.py ~1133-1150) send the legacy route to the canonical lane when the product has declarations, and accept it there. The pin's package-request.json says "receivers MUST continue to accept legacy-only `format_ids`" and "Sellers MUST normalize every entry through the canonical mapping path before product satisfaction checks". So a legacy 3.x buyer selecting display_300x250 on a canonical-only 300x250 image product is refused, while the same buyer is accepted on a dual-arm product. I found this by reading the code and did not execute it.
  • No scenario grades that legacy-selector-on-canonical-only case. @T-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION-canonical-only-purchasable (feature:693) tests only the exact_300x250 and product_option_ref selectors. The legacy_display_300x250 row in @T-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION accepted (feature:56) uses given_declaration, which stores a DUAL-ARM product (uc_format_satisfaction.py:307-315, with_legacy_arm=True), so it cannot see this. The gap mainly belongs to P2/E2/L4 (the selector contract). It also qualifies the grade's use of 'purchasable' as evidence for S2: canonical-only products are purchasable through canonical selectors, not through the legacy selector the pin still requires sellers to accept.
  • docs/rfcs/002-creative-format-model.md:692-693 cites stale line numbers (models.py:390, products.py:570, models.py:2082, models.py:509). HEAD has them at 423, 587, 2120 and 543. This is documentation drift and does not affect the grade.

S3 IMPLEMENTED Publish reusable publisher options in /.well-known/adagents.json and reference them by {publisher_domain, format_option_id}

2026-10-28: PARTIAL (grader PARTIAL, skeptic PARTIAL). 2026-10-29: PARTIAL. 2026-10-30: IMPLEMENTED. Graded on all four transports: yes. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.3.

"3. Publish reusable publisher options in `/.well-known/adagents.json` and reference them with `publisher_domain` plus `format_option_id`." (canonical-formats-migration.mdx, Seller migration). Normative strength comes from adagents.json#/properties/formats: "Products selling this publisher's inventory SHOULD reference these declarations by `format_option_id` on the placement or via inline `format_options` whose `format_option_id` matches an entry here". Related: core/product.json format_options: "an unresolved publisher placement or format-option reference MUST fail closed".

docs/creative/canonical-formats-migration.mdx § Seller migration, step 3 (line 26 at v3.2.7); docs/creative/formats.mdx § The three authorities (lines 11-19) and § Canonical declaration (lines 42-43); static/schemas/source/adagents.json#/properties/formats (line 153-155); static/schemas/source/core/product-format-declaration.json#/properties/publisher_domain. Since the previous audit: unchanged. The step 3 wording is the same at v3.2.7, and formats.mdx still lists "Publisher adagents.json.formats[]" as the publisher-acceptance authority. The audit wrote "reference them by" where the pin says "reference them with"; the meaning is the same. At v3.2.7 the strongest normative form is a SHOULD (adagents.json formats description), and core/product.json adds a MUST fail-closed for an unresolved publisher format-option reference.

Graded

Yes. tests/bdd/features/local-publisher-format-catalog.feature passes on a2a/mcp/rest/e2e_rest in innet_291026_2041: T-PUBFORMATS-published, T-PUBFORMATS-no-catalog and T-PUBFORMATS-reference-resolves, one node each per transport. The Thens are not vacuous. They read the served adagents.json and validate it against the pinned adagents.json schema. reference-resolves reads the product option's publisher_domain and format_option_id off the get_products wire, fetches that publisher's adagents.json and resolves the option there with the same format_kind. The steps are in tests/bdd/steps/domain/publisher_format_catalog.py:123-186. Related rows also pass on all four: T-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION-refused rows wrong_publisher_domain and wrong_publisher_option_id, and T-UC-GET-PRODUCTS-format-option-refs-filter.

Evidence

Publish: src/core/signing/trust_root.py:268-269 emits formats[] from tenants.publisher_format_options (models.py:176, validated at :316-324 through src/core/publisher_format_catalog.py). Reference: an own-namespace option must resolve in the catalog, enforced by PublisherCatalog.refuse_unresolved (publisher_format_catalog.py:116) at src/admin/blueprints/products.py:1341/1538, inventory_profiles.py:217/433 and authorized_properties.py:413. Commits cfda206fa, a0eede322 and 3d0063b6f. Pin: canonical-formats-migration.mdx § Seller migration step 3 at v3.2.7, and adagents.json#/properties/formats at v3.2.7 (a SHOULD).

Gaps

The seller-side refusal of an own-namespace option that does not resolve (authoring a product or profile, withdrawing a catalog entry, linking a profile) is covered only by tests/admin, not by BDD. The product in reference-resolves is seeded through ProductFactory. Nothing checks references at serve time; the catalog check runs where declarations are authored. Another publisher's catalog is resolved by the buyer under core/placement.json, so that is not a seller gap.

Beads

  • salesagent-ol033.20.3 (close): Both halves the pin asks for are built and graded on all four transports: publishing formats[], and products referencing it by {publisher_domain, format_option_id} so that the reference resolves. The admin-side refusal guard goes beyond the step and is covered in tests/admin. If the owner wants that guard in BDD, it should be its own follow-up.

Code

  • src/core/signing/trust_root.py:248 build_adagents_json starts the served document as {$schema, authorized_agents: []} and adds only authorized_agents entries and last_updated (lines 248-275). It never emits a top-level formats[], so no reusable publisher option is ever published.
  • src/routes/well_known.py:157 _adagents_json_handler builds the served /.well-known/adagents.json from authorized properties and signing keys only (lines 157-172). The route is registered at line 217. Product format_options are not read, so nothing feeds a formats[] catalog.
  • src/core/database/repositories/authorized_property.py:45 list_for_publisher_domain is the only data source for the served adagents.json: AuthorizedProperty rows for the tenant's host. No format catalog is stored or queried.
  • src/core/database/models.py:433 Product.format_options is a JSONType column holding pinned ProductFormatDeclaration documents, so a product can carry {publisher_domain, format_option_id} publisher-scoped declarations.
  • src/admin/blueprints/products.py:116 _parse_authored_format_options validates authored declarations only against the pinned type (line 141). A publisher_domain/format_option_id pair is not resolved against that publisher's adagents.json formats[], so an unresolved publisher reference is accepted instead of failing closed as core/product.json requires.
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:593 product_format_option_identities builds FormatOptionIdentity(publisher_domain, format_option_id) from a product's declarations (lines 593-626), so a reference is compared as a whole publisher-or-product scoped identity. This is the reference half, implemented.
  • src/core/tools/products.py:606 The get_products format_option_refs filter compares whole identities (product_format_option_identities(...) & requested_options), so a publisher-scoped ref does not match a product-local option with the same id.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:272 _option_identity derives the publisher/product scope from the presence of publisher_domain, so a create_media_buy format_option_ref must match both publisher_domain and format_option_id.
  • src/core/database/mapping/creative.py:130 A creative's format_option_ref (including a publisher-scope ref) is persisted as the stored option document and echoed back on list_creatives (line 237).

Code

  • src/core/product_conversion.py:488 `if product_model.format_options: product_data["format_options"] = product_model.format_options`: the stored canonical declarations are handed to the Product DTO. The DTO validates them into the pinned 16-branch ProductFormatDeclaration union, and the key is omitted when nothing is stored. Nothing is derived from format_ids.
  • src/core/product_conversion.py:497 Refuses a product that has neither format_ids nor format_options. This is the anyOf in core/product.json, so a canonical-only product is servable.
  • src/core/product_conversion.py:577 Placements are passed through as stored (with legacy kind/mode defaults). A placement's inline format_options, which carry the placement-specific layer, reach the wire untouched. Nothing checks that they narrow the product's upper bound.
  • src/core/tools/products.py:386 _get_products_impl converts every tenant product through convert_product_model_to_schema. A conversion failure becomes AdCPInternalError at :391-398.
  • src/core/tools/products.py:587 The format_kinds filter (:587-590) and the format_option_refs filter (:606-609) select products on their stored format_options. This is the read side that makes the returned options usable.
  • src/core/tools/products.py:862 GetProductsResponse is built from the eligible Product models, so format_options is serialized at the wire edge. There is no `fields` projection that could drop it.
  • src/admin/blueprints/products.py:883 Admin product creation parses the canonical format_options against the DTO's pinned type and refuses a product with no format arm (:887). New products are authored canonically.
  • src/core/database/models.py:433 products.format_options is a nullable JSONType column, so a canonical-only product is representable.
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:195 Stale docstring: it says v1_format_ref needs Product.format_options[] "which this seller does not carry yet". The seller does carry them now. Documentation rot only.

Gaps

  • The baseline innet_181026_1634 ran at commit 578c00795 with adcp spec 3.2.3 (meta.json adcp.spec_version). HEAD has since re-pinned to adcp==9.3.0 / v3.2.7 (d15f908de). No scenario in BR-UC-GET-PRODUCTS-format-options stores format_option_id or publisher_domain, so nothing asserts that the identifiers buyers use in format_option_refs survive announcement. The format_option_refs filter scenario (BR-UC-GET-PRODUCTS-canonical-format-filters.feature:54) asserts which product is selected, not the announced declaration's content (uc_get_products_canonical_format_filters.py:211-216).
  • The placement-specific layer is ungraded and unvalidated. src/core/product_conversion.py:577-585 passes stored placements[].format_options through, but nothing checks that they stay within the product's upper bound, which core/placement.json says they MUST NOT exceed. No BDD scenario stores a placement with inline format_options. RFC 002 Part 11 lists placement layering as ungraded and unbuilt.
  • No account-specific narrowing exists. get_products returns each product's stored format_options for every caller. The pin says these constraints 'belong there' but does not require a seller to have any, so this is a limitation, not a defect.
  • No inventory-profile canonical arm. InventoryProfile has format_ids only (salesagent-8qela.154, cited at src/core/errors/details.py:304). Inventory-level constraints can reach get_products only through the deprecated arm, or by authoring format_options on each product.
  • Feature comments over-claim storyboard grading. BR-UC-GET-PRODUCTS-format-options.feature:28-30 and :162-165 say the canonical_formats storyboard grades the divergence shape. At v3.2.7, static/compliance/source/protocols/media-buy/scenarios/canonical_formats.yaml:7-8 has `requires: [controller]`, and this seller has no comply_test_controller, so the storyboard grades nothing here. Only the BDD scenarios grade S4.
  • Stale RFC and code text. docs/rfcs/002-creative-format-model.md:719-724 lists 4 scenarios for this feature (there are 9). :733-736 says deleting the route row would 'auto-xfail', but unrouted scenarios are now refused (tests/unit/test_architecture_bdd_unrouted_is_refused.py). :745 says no scenario asserts a get_products errors[] advisory, but the divergent scenario at feature:150 does. tests/bdd/conftest.py:6778 cites the deleted test_architecture_measurement_floors.py. src/core/format_resolver.py:195-197 says the seller does not carry format_options yet.
  • Dynamic signal-variant products are ungraded. get_products appends variants from generate_variants_for_brief (src/core/tools/products.py:461-475), and the variants copy format_options from their template (src/services/dynamic_products.py:337-338, the fix for salesagent-ki6a4). ProductEnv patches generate_variants_for_brief out, though (tests/harness/product.py:82, EXTERNAL_PATCHES 'dynamic_variants'), so no BDD scenario confirms that a variant's announced options reach the wire.
  • Non-admin creation paths still author format_ids-only new products: src/core/database/database.py:311 (CREATE_SAMPLE_DATA), scripts/setup/seed_products.py:48, and src/adapters/mock_ad_server.py:1595 (format_ids written from the mock product-config form). The pin says 'Every new 3.2 product declares its closed accepted set in Product.format_options[]'.
  • The product-level composition rules in core/product.json format_options are unbuilt and ungraded. The rules are: product format_options is the union and upper bound over placements, and a multi-publisher product without placements[] MUST announce the common intersection. The seller announces stored declarations verbatim and checks none of this. The colleague covered the placement layer but not the multi-publisher intersection rule.
  • Only one step reads announced format_options on the wire: git grep finds the single read at tests/bdd/steps/domain/uc_get_products_format_options.py:76. No other feature (canonical-format-filters, format_satisfaction) asserts the content of an announced declaration. This reinforces the colleague's single-option and identity-member gaps.

2026-10-28: PARTIAL (grader PARTIAL, skeptic PARTIAL). 2026-10-29: PARTIAL. 2026-10-30: PARTIAL. Wave 8 (tree ef4d8f5fa): PARTIAL. Wave 9 (tree 6058b2d33): IMPLEMENTED. Graded on all four transports: pending box run. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.5.

"An agent-global format list cannot express product-, placement-, account-, or inventory-specific deliverability. For that reason, sales-agent `list_creative_formats` is deprecated in 3.2." (sales-agent-creative-capabilities.mdx:26) / "Existing 3.x agents MAY continue serving the task for compatibility. New implementations MUST use the canonical surfaces below and SHOULD derive any legacy response mechanically from canonical declarations rather than maintaining a second catalog." and "Sales agents MUST NOT use an agent-global format list as a purchasability promise." (task-reference/list_creative_formats.mdx) / "Named `format_id` objects and `list_creative_formats` are deprecated compatibility surfaces." (formats.mdx:9)

v3.2.7:docs/creative/sales-agent-creative-capabilities.mdx § Format deliverability is product-scoped (line 26) and § Compatibility (line 92); v3.2.7:docs/creative/task-reference/list_creative_formats.mdx (preamble, § Use the surface that owns the fact); v3.2.7:docs/creative/formats.mdx line 9; v3.2.7:docs/media-buy/specification.mdx § Canonical creative-format discovery (lines 640-656); v3.2.7:static/schemas/source/media-buy/list-creative-formats-request.json#/deprecated (true, x-deprecated-in 3.2.0); v3.2.7:static/schemas/source/media-buy/list-creative-formats-response.json#/deprecated. Since the previous audit: Wording differs from the audit row. The pin never says "stop advertising" and names no "required" status to withdraw. At v3.2.7 the task is deprecated for sales agents, and an existing 3.x agent MAY keep serving it for compatibility. What binds: new implementations MUST use the canonical surfaces (Product.format_options[] for deliverability). Sales agents MUST NOT use an agent-global format list as a purchasability promise. The deprecated response schema says it "is not a sales-agent deliverability contract". No v3.2.7 protocol or specialism storyboard requires the task. The only storyboards that touch it are universal/pagination-integrity-creative-formats.yaml and read-tool-idempotency.yaml, and both gate on requires_tool and phrase it as "voluntarily exposes". So the obligation is now "present it only as a deprecated compatibility surface, never as the seller's catalogue or a prerequisite", not "stop exposing it". The audit's structural evidence holds at the pin: core/format-id.json carries "deprecated": true, and so do both media-buy list-creative-formats schemas. The "derived, not authored" half belongs to F4.

Wave 9, read at 6058b2d33

Built: list_creative_formats lists a format only when some product's served contract (Product.format_contract) names it, with the definition its creative agent, or the in-process adapter, publishes (a65287a42). A format an agent publishes that no product declares is not listed. A declared reference that no agent defines is omitted, not invented. A listed entry is matched to a declaration by CreativeAgentRegistry.lists_format, the rule get_format uses (6058b2d33). The MCP tool and the A2A skill advertise the pinned request schema's description, which marks the task deprecated, in place of the SDK's "Returns format_ids needed for sync_creatives" (4b04ccb02, through ToolSpec.advertised_description). F1 IMPLEMENTED Canonical compatibility is directional and exact — exact dimensions/durations match; a requested range must be contained , overlap insufficient; required slots present with compatible asset types; an exact publisher claim matches both halves. formats.mdx § Matching and satisfaction

2026-10-28: PARTIAL (grader PARTIAL, skeptic PARTIAL). 2026-10-29: IMPLEMENTED. 2026-10-30: IMPLEMENTED. Graded on all four transports: yes. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.6.

Canonical compatibility is directional. A creative capability or manifest satisfies a target only when it meets every target constraint: - Exact dimensions and durations must match. - A requested range must be contained within the accepted range; overlap is insufficient. - Required slots must be present with compatible asset types. - An exact publisher claim must match both `publisher_domain` and `format_option_id`. - Equal `format_kind` values alone do not prove compatibility.

docs/creative/formats.mdx § Matching and satisfaction (v3.2.7, lines 101-108); reinforced by docs/creative/canonical-formats.mdx § Format matching vs product satisfaction (v3.2.7, lines 1492-1514) and static/schemas/source/media-buy/package-request.json#/description ("A single resolved route that does not satisfy the product is rejected with `UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE`"). Since the previous audit: unchanged. formats.mdx has no diff between v3.2.3 and v3.2.7, and canonical-formats.mdx and product-format-declaration.json are also unchanged. package-request.json gained one line, but the selector contract is unchanged. The audit's paraphrase matches the pin's four rules. The pin's fifth bullet ("Equal format_kind values alone do not prove compatibility") is implied by the audit row but not quoted in it.

Graded

Graded, all passed on a2a/mcp/rest/e2e_rest in innet_291026_2041. The run is at 59449acee, the parent of c20a4c2c1, and the only commit between them touches none of these files. Selector side, BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature: @T-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION-accepted (:74, 19 rows) and -refused (:169, 26 rows), which cover exact dims, exact duration, duration_ms_exact over duration_ms_range precedence, contained_range, overlapping_range, range_over_upper_bound, slot_missing, slot_wrong_asset_type, wrong_publisher_domain and wrong_publisher_option_id; -conflicting and -conflicting-option-sets, 4 rows each. Manifest side: -manifest-sync-refused (9 rows) and -manifest-sync-accepted (13), -manifest-create-size-mismatch (:1968), -manifest-update-size-mismatch (:1980) and -manifest-option-ref-unpublished (:1832). The Thens are real: accept asserts wire packages[0].product_id, media_buy_id and an absent errors key (uc_format_satisfaction.py:1949). Refuse calls assert_wire_error('UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE', field=packages[0].<arm>) (:1971). Manifest refusals assert the code and the problem path with its stated and accepted values.

Evidence

src/core/format_satisfaction.py:568 _range_unsatisfied (both ends contained; overlap fails); :591 _MUST_BE_STATED + :603 _constraint_unsatisfied (exact numbers equal; silence about width/height/duration fails); :651 _unsatisfied_params (directional; duration_ms_exact > duration_ms_range; exact-in-range cross-key); :444 _slot_shortfall (group AND asset_type); :1319 _option_route (FormatOptionIdentity equality = both halves); :1346 _kind_route (all same-kind candidates graded); :1267 _graded (manifest graded via _manifest_shortfalls); :720 _ASSET_FACTS; :2512 creative_satisfies. Call sites: media_buy_create.py:918 (selector gate), :550 and :4467 (creative gates); media_buy_update.py:628; creatives/_assignments.py:295. Spec: formats.mdx § Matching and satisfaction at v3.2.7 (5 bullets, verified via gh at ref v3.2.7); product-format-declaration.json 'Format matching vs satisfaction (normative)'.

Gaps

The code path exists (_ASSET_FACTS video/audio duration_ms -> duration_ms_exact, crossed to duration_ms_range in _unsatisfied_params). That row belongs to the manifest-validation obligation M3 (ol033.20.14), not F1. Refusal rows assert code and field only, not details.capability, so a refusal for the wrong reason on the same arm would still pass. Each refused row has an accept control that differs only in the selector, so I don't count that as vacuous.

The 2026-10-28 grade (tree 5bf5677fb, outcomes from innet_181026_1634)

Grader: PARTIAL. Skeptic: PARTIAL (disagrees). Applies to this seller: yes (This is a sales agent that publishes Product.format_options[]. The seller checks product satisfaction twice: on create_media_buy package selectors (format_option_refs, format_kind+params, and the legacy format_ids), and on creatives it accepts onto packages through create_media_buy, update_media_buy and sync_creatives assignments. canonical-formats.mdx makes the seller side explicit: "Sellers and SDKs MUST normalize legacy named formats before comparing", and "the requested creative or package selector MUST declare and match that constraint". The creative-agent capability half of the rule does not apply here.). Graded on all four transports: yes. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.6.

The pin's text in formats.mdx § Matching and satisfaction is unchanged at v3.2.7, and it applies to this seller. The audit's ABSENT is out of date. src/core/format_satisfaction.py now implements directional satisfaction for create_media_buy package selectors, wired at media_buy_create.py:805-833: exact dimensions must match, a requested range must be contained (overlap fails), required slots must match on group and type, both halves of an option identity must match, a bare kind is refused, and legacy ids are normalized before comparison. BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature's accepted outline (line 56) and refused outline (line 101) grade this with wire assertions (UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE plus the field path), and every row passed on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest in the baseline. Since the baseline only comments changed in the feature, and nothing changed in the steps or production. The obligation is PARTIAL for two reasons. First, the manifest side is not implemented: creatives are graded on kind and option membership only (params=None, deferred to salesagent-ol033.7). Second, several sub-rules are ungraded: exact duration, a right-domain/wrong-option publisher claim, a right-group/wrong-type slot, and an over-the-upper-bound range. The canonical_formats storyboard grades nothing here, because comply_test_controller is out of scope for this seller.

Code

  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:453 _range_unsatisfied: tests both ends of a requested range against the accepted window (containment, so overlap alone fails), and a scalar for membership.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:494 _constraint_unsatisfied: any other numeric bound is an exact dimension or duration and must be equal; silence fails (bare `image` vs fixed 300x250 is refused). max_/min_ are bounds, lists are allowed sets.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:330 _slot_shortfall: each required slot must be offered as the same (asset_group_id, asset_type) pair. A claim that declares no slots is deliberately unconstrained (lines 337-342 name this as a ceiling).
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:517 _unsatisfied_params: grades only the target's constraints, which makes it directional; applies the pin's duration_ms_exact > duration_ms_range precedence.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:710 _option_route: an option ref selects the declaration whose FormatOptionIdentity (publisher_domain + format_option_id) is equal, so both halves of a publisher claim must match; otherwise OPTION_NOT_PUBLISHED.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:723 _kind_route: closed-set check on format_kind, then grades params against EVERY same-kind declaration with exact=False, so an equal kind alone is not proof of compatibility.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:608 _legacy_route: a legacy format_id is normalized (v1_format_ref lookup, then upgrade_legacy_format_id projection of width/height/duration_ms) and the projected params are graded by the same rules before comparison.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:805 create_media_buy package gate: resolve_routes over every selector route against the product's declarations; CONFLICTING_SELECTORS at :816, UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE via canonical_refusal at :828 with the arm's field path.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:678 _graded: `if params is None: return resolved`. A stored creative's identity carries no params, so satisfaction for creatives is kind/option membership only (module docstring lines 43-50 defer manifest-side grading to salesagent-ol033.7).
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:4239 Creative gate on create calls satisfies(stored_identity.as_selector(), ...) with no params, so a creative's asset dimensions and slots are not graded against the canonical declaration.
  • src/core/tools/creatives/_assignments.py:262 sync_creatives assignment gate: same satisfies() call with no params, so it checks kind/option membership only.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_update.py:495 update_media_buy creative gate: same satisfies() call with no params.

Gaps

  • Production gap: manifest-side satisfaction is missing. Creatives reach satisfies() with params=None (src/core/format_satisfaction.py:678; callers src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:4239, src/core/tools/creatives/_assignments.py:262, src/core/tools/media_buy_update.py:495), so a canonical `image` creative whose assets are 728x90 would be accepted onto a product fixing 300x250, against the pin's 'Equal format_kind values alone do not prove compatibility' and 'a creative capability or manifest satisfies a target only when it meets every target constraint'. This is deferred to salesagent-ol033.7 per src/core/format_satisfaction.py:43-50.
  • Production ceiling: a selector or claim declaring no slots is treated as satisfying required slots (src/core/format_satisfaction.py:337-345). This is acceptable for a package selector but leaves 'Required slots must be present' unenforced on any creative path.
  • Missing scenario: exact DURATION matching. No declaration in _DECLARATIONS (tests/bdd/steps/domain/uc_format_satisfaction.py:66-131) uses duration_ms_exact, so the 'exact durations must match' half of rule 1 and the duration_ms_exact > duration_ms_range precedence (src/core/format_satisfaction.py:517) are ungraded. No feature under tests/bdd/features mentions duration_ms_exact.
  • Missing scenario row: publisher claim with the RIGHT publisher_domain and a WRONG format_option_id. Only the domain half is graded (wrong_publisher_domain, uc_format_satisfaction.py:194); unpublished_option_ref is product-scoped, so 'must match both publisher_domain and format_option_id' is graded on one half for publisher scope.
  • Missing scenario row: required slot with the RIGHT asset_group_id and a WRONG asset_type. slot_missing (uc_format_satisfaction.py:188) differs in both group (caption vs video_main) and type, so 'compatible asset types' is not graded independently of slot presence.
  • Missing scenario row: range endpoint containment on the upper bound (e.g. [10000, 90000] against [5000, 60000]). overlapping_range only exceeds the lower bound, so a seller testing only the low end would pass.
  • No e2e_rest realisation gaps: every selector-satisfaction row passed on e2e_rest in innet_181026_1634, and no row is listed in tests/bdd/e2e_rest_known_failures.txt or xfail-routed in tests/bdd/conftest.py.
  • Production: a format_kind selector that says nothing about duration is accepted against a declaration with duration_ms_range (format_satisfaction.py:453-462, 534-541). The pin requires the request to 'declare and satisfy' a duration_ms_range constraint. No scenario covers it: the only range rows (contained_range, overlapping_range) both state a range.
  • Production: a claim with duration_ms_exact outside the declaration's duration_ms_range is accepted, because only the claim's duration_ms_range key is read. The pin says an exact value satisfies a range only when it falls inside the interval.
  • Missing scenario: a bare video_hosted selector (no duration) against the duration_range declaration. It is the duration counterpart of bare_image_kind, and production would accept it today.
  • Missing scenario: a duration_ms_exact claim against a range declaration, with one value inside the range and one outside.
  • Creative path: a creative with a legacy format_id is graded on that format_id's projection or parameters, not on its assets. A creative asserting display_300x250 whose assets are 728x90 would still be accepted. The pin's canonical-formats.mdx example table (for instance '300x250, no pixel_ratios | 600x500 image -> Reject') is manifest-side and is not implemented.

Skeptic's corrections

  • The package-selector side is not complete, though the colleague's summary presents it as complete. The range rule ignores silence: _range_unsatisfied (src/core/format_satisfaction.py:453-462, docstring 'Silence is not a request and so cannot fail one') lets a format_kind selector that says nothing about duration satisfy a declaration with duration_ms_range. I checked this in the repo venv: _unsatisfied_params({}, {'duration_ms_range':[5000,60000]}) returns [] (accepted). The pin requires the opposite. v3.2.7 core/product-format-declaration.json 'Format matching vs satisfaction (normative)' says: 'when this declaration specifies fixed constraints such as width, height, duration_ms_exact, or duration_ms_range, a buyer request or creative manifest MUST declare and satisfy those constraints. A broad request with no dimensions or duration does not satisfy a fixed-size or fixed-duration product'. canonical-formats.mdx § Format matching vs product satisfaction says the same: 'An under-specified request is not a wildcard for product gating'.
  • There is a second production deviation on the package path: an exact duration outside the accepted range is accepted. _unsatisfied_params({'duration_ms_exact':90000}, {'duration_ms_range':[5000,60000]}) returns [] (checked in the repo venv), because the range is looked up under the claim's duration_ms_range key only (format_satisfaction.py:534-541). The pin says 'An exact value satisfies a range when the exact value falls inside the accepted interval', so a value outside the interval must fail.
  • The claim 'creatives are graded on kind and option membership only' is imprecise. Creatives do pass params=None, and _graded skips params at format_satisfaction.py:678. But a creative that carries a legacy format_id goes through _legacy_route, because _canonical_lane (:803-806) sends any claim with an assertion there 'whatever else it carries'. _legacy_route then does a v1_format_ref lookup, or grades the projected width/height/duration_ms against the declarations (:597-640). On a deprecated-arm-only product, _legacy_lane (:860-866) applies format_is_supported plus the pixel_ratio supplement. So dimensions encoded in a creative's format_id ARE graded. What is not graded: the creative's actual assets (the manifest), and a canonical-kind-only creative.
  • Production keys creative satisfaction on format_id when one is present, which the colleague did not flag. Because the legacy route answers 'whatever else it carries' (format_satisfaction.py:798-806), a stored creative's retained format_id decides its satisfaction ahead of its canonical format_kind. The spec allows normalizing first, but this conflicts with the owner decision that format_id is never what business logic keys on.
  • The colleague missed several scenarios. All passed on a2a/mcp/rest/e2e_rest in innet_181026_1634. (a) BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature:438 sync-sized-conforming and :445 sync-sized-mismatched grade exact width/height matching of a creative's declared format_id parameters against a deprecated-arm product. (b) :469 density-mismatched and :476 density-silent grade pixel_ratio, including 'silence fails'. (c) The approval-gate-refuses/-accepts pair (around :612/:620) grades the bare_image_kind refusal on the manual-approval path. In production the selector gate now runs at media_buy_create.py:3174, before the approval branch at :3181, so the feature comment saying 'THESE SCENARIOS ARE RED ON PURPOSE' is stale. (d) The update-accepted/-refused scenarios (:311/:318) cover kind membership on update_media_buy. (e) The canonical-only purchasability outline covers a product with no legacy arm.
  • Two stale details in the feature: the header comment (line 10) still says '3.2.3, the pinned version', and one @source line cites dist/compliance/3.2.3/..., an older bundle. The rule text is identical at v3.2.7 (I confirmed git diff v3.2.3 v3.2.7 is empty for formats.mdx, canonical-formats.mdx and product-format-declaration.json), so this does not change the grade.

Code

  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:20 Module docstring cites the fifth formats.mdx rule as part of its authority. Lines 45-50 state that a stored creative passes params=None, so the params rules are SKIPPED for creatives (deferred to salesagent-ol033.7).
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:723 _kind_route: closed-set check. Then every same-kind declaration is graded with exact=False, so the whole declaration is the obligation and staying silent about an exact dimension fails (lines 779-784). This also covers M2: it picks the declaration the claim satisfies, not the first one.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:494 _constraint_unsatisfied: an exact numeric bound fails when the claim is not a number or differs from it, so a bare `image` against width:300 fails.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:678 _graded: `if params is None: return resolved`. A claim that states no params (every stored creative) is satisfied by the selected declaration with no params comparison. For a canonical-kind-only creative, the selected declaration came from kind equality alone.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:853 _legacy_lane docstring: comparing kinds of projected legacy refs is explicitly declined because equal kind does not prove compatibility. The deprecated arm uses adcp format_is_supported with parameters plus pixel_ratio (line 865).
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:805 _resolve_package_selectors (def at :753): resolve_routes(selector_routes(format_kind, format_option_refs, params, format_ids)) per package. Raises CONFLICTING_SELECTORS (:816) or UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE via canonical_refusal (:828). This is the package-selector half of the obligation, and it is implemented.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:466 _resolved_format_verdicts: creative gate calls satisfies(claim.as_selector(), product.format_options, legacy_catalog=...) with no params, so a canonical-kind creative is graded on kind (closed set) only.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:627 Asset gate only checks that width/height EXIST (media_asset_requires_dimensions ... not width or not height). It never compares them to the selected declaration's width/height, so a 728x90 canonical image creative passes against a fixed 300x250 product.
  • src/core/tools/creatives/_assignments.py:262 sync_creatives assignment gate: satisfies(stored_identity.as_selector(), product.format_options, ...) with params=None. Same kind-only result for canonical creatives.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_update.py:495 update_media_buy creative gate: satisfies(stored.as_selector(), ...) with params=None. Same kind-only result.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:4239 A second create-path creative check, again satisfies(...) with no params.

Gaps

  • PRODUCTION GAP (creative/manifest side): the spec's sentence is 'A creative capability or manifest satisfies a target only when it meets every target constraint', and product-format-declaration.json says 'a buyer request or creative manifest MUST declare and satisfy those constraints'. A stored creative identified only by canonical format_kind (no format_option_ref, no legacy format_id) goes through _kind_route with params=None. _graded returns satisfied at src/core/format_satisfaction.py:678-679 whenever a declaration of the same kind exists, so equal format_kind alone decides compatibility, which is the inference F2 forbids. The asset gate at src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:627-631 checks only that width/height are present and never compares them to the selected declaration. Affected call sites: media_buy_create.py:466 and :4239, media_buy_update.py:495, creatives/_assignments.py:262. Example: a canonical image creative with a 728x90 asset assigned to a product fixing image 300x250 is accepted. The source defers this as salesagent-ol033.7 (format_satisfaction.py:45-50).
  • MISSING SCENARIO: nothing in tests/bdd/features grades the creative-side half. It needs a canonical `image` creative whose image_main asset is 728x90, assigned to the fixed_300x250 product on create_media_buy, update_media_buy and sync_creatives and refused, paired with the existing 300x250 accept rows (BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature:212, :323, :345). Those rows currently pass a kind-only seller because their only controls differ by kind (video_hosted).
  • WEAK SCENARIO PAIRING: @T-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION-creative-accepted (:212), -update-accepted (:323) and -sync-accepted (:345) are correct as written. But each is paired only with a different-kind refusal, so for creatives they grade the closed-set rule, not F2. The feature comment at :195-202 acknowledges this.
  • AUDIT CORRECTION: the audit's statement that storyboard steps reject_bare_image_selector_for_fixed_mrec / reject_image_width_without_height grade F2 does not hold for this seller. canonical_formats.yaml requires the comply_test_controller, which this seller does not include. reject_image_width_without_height is a schema-atomicity (INVALID_REQUEST) check, not F2. The BDD rows above replace the storyboard for the package-selector half.
  • MINOR CITATION: BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature:50 still cites the storyboard as `ref=v3.2.3 path=dist/compliance/3.2.3/...`, an older-version bundle path. At the pin the file is static/compliance/source/protocols/media-buy/scenarios/canonical_formats.yaml.
  • Which subject the pin's sentence names: the formats.mdx bullet (line 109 at v3.2.7) belongs to the sentence 'A creative capability or manifest satisfies a target only when it meets every target constraint'. Its own subject is the creative side, and that is the side not implemented. The implemented package-selector half rests mainly on product-format-declaration.json's 'a buyer request or creative manifest MUST declare and satisfy those constraints'. This does not change PARTIAL, but the RFC text should not cite the formats.mdx bullet as the authority for what the package gate implements.
  • The creative-side gap is reachable by buyers at the pin. core/creative-manifest.json at v3.2.7 declares `format_kind` as a top-level property, and only `assets` is required. A buyer can sync a creative with only a kind, which `CreativeFormatIdentity.from_row` (src/core/schemas/_base.py:803-811) loads with option=None and assertion=None. That creative reaches `_kind_route` with params=None, and `candidates[0]` is satisfied by kind alone (format_satisfaction.py:779-784 plus :678-679). A creative that carries a legacy format_id goes through `_legacy_route`/`_legacy_lane` instead, where parameters are compared, so the gap is limited to kind-only creatives.

F3 IMPLEMENTED For a multi-placement product the selected format set must cover every placement; reject or refine, never silently remove inventory. formats.mdx § Matching and satisfaction

2026-10-28: ABSENT (grader ABSENT, skeptic ABSENT). 2026-10-29: PARTIAL. 2026-10-30: PARTIAL. Wave 8 (tree ef4d8f5fa): PARTIAL. Wave 11 (tree ff5795e1a): IMPLEMENTED. Graded at 813ee8dff (innet_301026_1301): partly; on the wave 11 tree: pending box run. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.8.

For a product with multiple included placements, the selected format set must cover every placement. A seller must reject or refine a package with an uncovered placement; it must not silently remove inventory. If a multi-publisher product omits `placements[]`, each `publisher_properties[]` selector is an eligibility slice: product-local options apply to every slice, publisher-scoped options only to their publisher, and an indeterminate slice fails closed until the seller supplies placements or a refined product.

docs/creative/formats.mdx § Matching and satisfaction (line 111 at v3.2.7); restated normatively in static/schemas/source/core/product.json#/properties/format_options/description ("the selected creative set MUST cover every included placement; uncovered inventory MUST be rejected or refined, never silently omitted"). Since the previous audit: The first two sentences are the same in substance as the audit row ("must cover every placement; reject or refine, never silently remove inventory"). The pin adds a third sentence the audit row lacks: a multi-publisher product that omits placements[] is treated as per-publisher eligibility slices, and an indeterminate slice fails closed. core/product.json at the pin also states the intersection rule for one creative meant for every placement, and says per-placement creatives MAY use the union but the selected creative set MUST cover every included placement.

(2) The author-side clause of core/product.json ("MUST therefore be the common intersection"), by the owner's wave 11 decision to refuse the save and migrate the data: the admin product save refuses a publisher-scoped option on a multi-publisher product without placements[] (b0af09777), and data migration 7a74ba340f11 narrows stored rows to their common intersection (0dabf8f76). Creating an inventory profile is not checked, because a new profile has no linked products; linking one goes through the product save, which is checked.

Graded: @T-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION-placements-unresolved-no-selector, the covered outline's new publisher_ref_targetable | no_selector row, and @T-UC-GET-PRODUCTS-format-options-operator-common-intersection-accepted / -refused, collected on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest; plus tests/admin/test_product_format_options_authoring.py, tests/integration/test_product_repository.py and tests/integration/test_common_intersection_migration.py. The 2026-10-28 grade (tree 5bf5677fb, outcomes from innet_181026_1634)

Grader: ABSENT. Skeptic: ABSENT (agrees). Applies to this seller: yes (This rule binds the sales agent that sells the product. It is not a creative-agent rule. This seller stores per-product placements, each carrying its own format_ids (src/core/database/models.py:483-484), and publishes them on get_products (src/core/product_conversion.py:577-584, defaulting mode="targetable" at :348). So it can sell a product with several included placements, and it accepts package format selectors (format_option_refs / format_kind+params / format_ids) on create_media_buy. Its packages bind no placement subset (src/core/format_resolver.py:902-905), so every package covers all of a product's placements, and the coverage rule applies to every multi-placement product.). Graded on all four transports: no. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.8.

F3 is still an obligation at v3.2.7. formats.mdx § Matching and satisfaction says the selected format set for a multi-placement product must cover every placement, and the seller must reject or refine, never silently remove inventory. The pin adds a fail-closed rule for multi-publisher products without placements[], and core/product.json restates the coverage rule normatively. It applies to this seller, which stores and publishes per-placement format_ids and binds every package to all of a product's placements. It is ABSENT in production: create_media_buy's selector gate (media_buy_create.py:753) checks only product-level format_options and never reads placements, and the only placement validation (media_buy_update.py:1252) checks that placement ids exist. No BDD scenario grades it; the nearest ones (UC-003 invalid placement ids, FORMAT-SATISFACTION selector refusals) pass on all four transports but test different rules. So F3 is ungraded, and RFC 002 Part 11 itself lists it as 'Ungraded, and the behaviour is unbuilt'.

Code

  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:753 _resolve_package_selectors: the only package-level format gate on create. It resolves each package's selector routes against the product-level declarations only (declared_format_arms -> (format_options, format_ids), lines 798-812). It raises CONFLICTING_SELECTORS or UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE for selector disagreement or non-satisfaction. It never reads product.placements, so it cannot detect a placement that the selected format set leaves uncovered.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:923 selector_routes / SelectorResolution / resolve_routes (line 1107): the satisfaction machinery implements the package-request selector contract (resolve, equivalence, precedence) against product format_options. It has no placement dimension. The only 'placement' mention in the module (line 369) is the coordinated_placements kind's components, which is unrelated.
  • src/core/database/models.py:484 Product.placements JSONType column, documented as 'list of placement dicts (each with placement_id, name, description, format_ids)'. Per-placement format acceptance exists in the data model.
  • src/core/product_conversion.py:577 Stored placements are emitted on the wire Product, normalized to kind=seller_inline / mode=targetable (line 348). Multi-placement products therefore reach buyers.
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:902 Docstring: packages in this seller 'bind no placement subset' (placement_refs deliberately absent). Every package therefore includes all of a product's placements, so coverage of every placement is always required.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_update.py:1252 The only placement-aware validation in src/: update_media_buy checks that creative_assignments[].placement_ids exist on the product (lines 1252-1285). It checks only that the ids exist. It never checks that the selected or assigned format/creative set covers every placement.
  • src/core/schemas/product.py:61 Local Placement(LibraryPlacement) narrows format_ids to required. Product does not reference it, and only src/adapters/gam_inventory_discovery.py:985 constructs a Placement of that name. It plays no part in any coverage check.

The colleague cites only the first. Neither rule is implemented, so the status does not change, but any future scenario or implementation has to settle which text it grades against.
  • core/product.json at v3.2.7 also says an unresolved publisher placement or format-option reference MUST fail closed when a placement's effective set is computed (layer 3, publisher_ref). Nothing under src/ computes a per-placement effective set, so this sub-clause is absent too.

F4 IMPLEMENTED The compatibility projection must be DERIVED from canonical data and must not become a second authored catalog. sales-agent-creative-capabilities.mdx § Compatibility; canonical-formats-migration.mdx § Compatibility projection

2026-10-28: PARTIAL (grader PARTIAL, skeptic PARTIAL). 2026-10-29: PARTIAL. 2026-10-30: PARTIAL. Wave 8 (tree ef4d8f5fa): IMPLEMENTED. Graded on all four transports: partly. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.9.

sales-agent-creative-capabilities.mdx § Compatibility: "A sales agent may project canonical product declarations into deprecated `format_ids[]` and `list_creative_formats` for older 3.x peers. The projection must be derived; it must not become a second authored catalog." canonical-formats-migration.mdx: "They are not an alternative authoring model in 3.2: if exposed, they should be projections derived from canonical data." and § Compatibility projection: "Never merge independently authored legacy constraints back into the canonical catalog. That creates two sources of truth and makes compatibility decisions ambiguous." canonical-formats.mdx § Deprecated named-format compatibility projection (normative), Producer rules: "Canonical `format_options` are the source of truth. Compatibility `format_ids` MUST be derived from them rather than maintained as a second authored catalog." / "A producer that cannot guarantee agreement MUST omit the legacy compatibility projection." The same section also says "A producer MAY derive deprecated `format_ids` from those declarations only for a negotiated older-3.x compatibility response."

v3.2.7:docs/creative/sales-agent-creative-capabilities.mdx § Compatibility (line 92); v3.2.7:docs/creative/canonical-formats-migration.mdx (line 20; § Compatibility projection, lines 99-110); v3.2.7:docs/creative/canonical-formats.mdx § Deprecated named-format compatibility projection (normative) → Producer rules (lines 1603-1608); v3.2.7:static/schemas/source/core/product.json#/properties/format_ids. Since the previous audit: The wording of both cited sentences is unchanged at v3.2.7. The audit missed one source: canonical-formats.mdx § "Deprecated named-format compatibility projection (normative)" states the same rule as an uppercase MUST ("Compatibility `format_ids` MUST be derived from them rather than maintained as a second authored catalog"). The audit cited only the two lowercase "must" sentences.

  • src/admin/blueprints/products.py:169 _refuse_a_product_with_no_format_arm: the admin product surface no longer authors format_ids. The 2026-10-28 grade (tree 5bf5677fb, outcomes from innet_181026_1634)

Grader: IMPLEMENTED. Skeptic: IMPLEMENTED (agrees). Applies to this seller: yes (get_products is a sales-agent (media-buy) tool and ProductFilters is its request filter object. The DTO inherits both fields from the pinned library model, so a buyer can send them and gets them advertised.). Graded on all four transports: yes. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.10.

The audit's ABSENT verdict is out of date. At HEAD, get_products reads both canonical filters. filters.format_kinds keeps a product only if its format_options declare one of the requested kinds (src/core/tools/products.py:587). filters.format_option_refs keeps a product only if a declaration's exact scope-aware identity matches one of the refs (products.py:606, via FormatOptionIdentity.of_reference). The wording at v3.2.7 is the same as at 3.2.1. Two scenarios in BR-UC-GET-PRODUCTS-canonical-format-filters.feature (lines 33 and 54) each seed a counter-example product and assert exact product_id sets read off the wire. Both passed on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest in innet_181026_1634, so the obligation is IMPLEMENTED and graded. Remaining gaps: (1) the scenarios name only a single value, so the 'any of' OR semantics is not graded; (2) the publisher-scope ref arm is never positively selected; (3) a legacy-only product is never matched by format_kinds even when its format_id projects to the kind. That is arguably in tension with canonical-formats.mdx:1494 ('MUST normalize legacy named formats before comparing'), and this seller's list_creatives filter does that normalization. The canonical_formats and inline_creatives_without_sync storyboards require the controller and grade nothing here.

Code

  • src/core/tools/products.py:587 filters.format_kinds is read: products whose product_format_kinds(format_options) do not intersect the requested kinds are dropped (OR over the kinds, matching 'any of')
  • src/core/tools/products.py:606 filters.format_option_refs is read: each ref goes through FormatOptionIdentity.of_reference and a product is kept only if product_format_option_identities(format_options) intersects them (exact whole-identity match, scope-aware)
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:407 product_format_kinds: set of str(format_kind) over the product's format_options. An empty set means the product declares no canonical kind, so it is not selected. Legacy format_ids are not projected here.
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:593 product_format_option_identities: builds FormatOptionIdentity(publisher_domain, format_option_id) per declaration and skips declarations that name no format_option_id
  • src/core/schemas/_base.py:621 FormatOptionIdentity.of_reference reads publisher_domain only when scope == 'publisher', so a product-scope ref never matches a publisher-catalog option with the same id
  • src/core/product_conversion.py:479 format_options are served as stored and never derived from format_ids. A legacy-only product therefore has no canonical kind for the format_kinds filter to match.
  • src/core/tools/products.py:534 The filter block runs on every get_products path before policy, min_exposures and ranking. Nothing downstream adds back a product it removed (ranking can only remove more).

Gaps

  • Scenario gap: no scenario names more than one value in filters.format_kinds or filters.format_option_refs. The pin's 'any of these' (OR) semantics is not graded, so an AND or first-element-only implementation would still pass. See tests/bdd/features/BR-UC-GET-PRODUCTS-canonical-format-filters.feature:33 and :54, and steps tests/bdd/steps/domain/uc_get_products_canonical_format_filters.py:143,157.
  • Scenario gap: no scenario selects a product with a publisher-scoped ref {scope:'publisher', publisher_domain, format_option_id}. Nothing checks that publisher_domain must match exactly (wrong domain excluded, right domain selected). Only the product-scope arm is named, at uc_get_products_canonical_format_filters.py:157.
  • Possible production gap (interpretive): a legacy-only product (format_ids and no format_options), which core/product.json still permits during the 3.x window, is never selected by filters.format_kinds, even when its legacy format_id projects to the requested kind. product_format_kinds reads only format_options (src/core/format_resolver.py:407), and product_conversion never derives format_options from format_ids (src/core/product_conversion.py:479). canonical-formats.mdx:1494 @ v3.2.7 says 'Sellers and SDKs MUST normalize legacy named formats before comparing them to canonical declarations'. This seller's list_creatives format_kinds filter does project legacy refs (graded by BR-UC-018-list-creatives.feature:1099), so the two filters behave differently. No scenario seeds a legacy-only product against the canonical product filter.
  • Documentation gap: the feature header (BR-UC-GET-PRODUCTS-canonical-format-filters.feature:8 and its @source lines 26-27) names only format_kinds and standard_formats_only. There is no @source line for product-filters.json property=format_option_refs, although the scenario at :54 grades it.
  • No storyboard grades this here. The pin's inline_creatives_without_sync.yaml (which sends filters.format_kinds:['image']) and canonical_formats.yaml both declare requires: [controller], and comply_test_controller is not part of this seller. Grading therefore rests on the BDD scenarios alone.
  • The legacy-only gap is wider than one hypothetical product shape, and it has a structural cause. InventoryProfile has no format_options column and keeps format_ids NOT NULL (models.py:2120). Every profile-linked product's format arm therefore comes from the profile's legacy format_ids (effective_format_ids, models.py:543), and product_format_kinds reads only the product's own format_options. So any profile-linked product without its own format_options column, plus every pre-canonical product (ProductFactory still defaults to legacy-only), drops out of every format_kinds and format_option_refs result. Against canonical-formats.mdx 'Format matching vs product satisfaction' @ v3.2.7 ('Sellers and SDKs MUST normalize legacy named formats before comparing them to canonical declarations'), this is a false-negative direction. The owner decision (format_id is never what business logic keys on) and product_conversion.py:479's refusal to synthesize declarations make it a deliberate position, not an accident. I am leaving it as interpretive, the same as the colleague.
  • The format_option_refs filter reads only product-level format_options. Per core/product.json format_options @ v3.2.7, a placement may carry its own inline declarations and publisher_ref catalog narrowing. An option id named only at placement or adagents.json catalog level is never matched. Product-level options are the stated upper bound, so this is an edge case, and nothing grades it.

P2 IMPLEMENTED Honour package-level canonical selectors format_kind / format_option_refs on create_media_buy and update_media_buy

2026-10-28: PARTIAL (grader PARTIAL, skeptic PARTIAL). 2026-10-29: IMPLEMENTED. 2026-10-30: IMPLEMENTED. Graded on all four transports: yes. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.11.

package-request.json description: "Format selector contract (normative). New 3.2 buyers author at most one canonical selector route: `format_option_refs`, or `format_kind` with optional `params`; omitting both selects all formats supported by the product. ... Receivers process selectors in this order: (1) independently resolve each present route to canonical declarations, without applying route precedence; an unresolved `format_option_ref` or a legacy `format_id` that cannot be normalized through the canonical mapping path MUST be rejected with `UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE`; (2) when every route resolves, derive the product `format_options[]` entries selected by each route using directional product satisfaction, and require the selected option sets to match; ... (3) reject different format shapes, selected option sets, or incompatible dimensions with `CONFLICTING_SELECTORS`; and only then (4) apply precedence: `format_option_refs`, then `format_kind` plus `params`, then `format_ids`. ... A single resolved route that does not satisfy the product is rejected with `UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE`." For update, package-update.json: "Fully-immutable fields (product_id, format_ids, format_option_refs, format_kind, params, pricing_option_id) cannot appear in update payloads — schema-enforced via the `not` constraint at the root of this object." update-media-buy-request.json new_packages items $ref package-request.json.

static/schemas/source/media-buy/package-request.json#/description (v3.2.7); static/schemas/source/media-buy/package-update.json#/description and #/not; static/schemas/source/media-buy/update-media-buy-request.json#/properties/new_packages; docs/media-buy/task-reference/create_media_buy.mdx § Format Specification (lines 1162-1191) and § Package Object (lines 229-232); docs/media-buy/task-reference/update_media_buy.mdx line 836. Since the previous audit: The create-side contract is the same. Two things moved. (1) The audit's "honour on update_media_buy" no longer reads correctly at the pin. A package-update must NOT carry format_option_refs, format_kind or params (the root `not` lists all three next to format_ids). Selectors reach update_media_buy only through new_packages[], which use the package-request contract. (2) Between v3.2.3 and v3.2.7, canonical_formats.yaml gained `requires: [controller]`, so the storyboard steps the audit cites grade nothing on this seller, which has no comply_test_controller. The direct-selector sample also gained image_formats in params. The audit's evidence ("one grep hit, a comment") is stale: src now resolves both routes.
