The 2026-10-29 grade (tree 1b5c6880b, outcomes from innet_291026_0257)

Both halves are built and graded. On create_media_buy the selector routes are resolved independently, compared as selected option sets, refused with UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE or CONFLICTING_SELECTORS in the pin's order, and recorded; legacy selectors against canonical-only and legacy-only products are covered. On update_media_buy a package update carrying any of the six fully-immutable fields (format_option_refs, format_kind and params among them) is refused INVALID_REQUEST under keyword not, read off the pinned schema, and new_packages is refused UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE because this seller does not add packages to a live buy; add_packages is no longer advertised. The pin allows UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE there as the legacy code and prefers ACTION_NOT_ALLOWED.

The 2026-10-28 grade (tree 5bf5677fb, outcomes from innet_181026_1634)

Grader: PARTIAL. Skeptic: PARTIAL (disagrees). Applies to this seller: yes (The receiver obligation sits on create_media_buy and update_media_buy package-requests, which a sales agent serves. It does not depend on comply_test_controller. Only its storyboard does.). Graded on all four transports: no. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.11.

The create_media_buy half is implemented and graded. The update_media_buy half is not. Since the audit, src gained a full selector resolver (format_satisfaction.selector_routes/resolve_routes), called once before both approval branches (media_buy_create.py:3174). It resolves format_option_refs, format_kind+params and format_ids independently, refuses unresolved or unsatisfied routes with UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE at packages[i].<arm>, refuses disagreeing routes with CONFLICTING_SELECTORS at packages[i], and records the winning declaration as formats_to_provide. BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION grades this with wire-value Thens. Its accepted, refused, conflicting, snapshot and canonical-only outlines include the two storyboard-equivalent cases (product_option_ref, exact_300x250 direct selector) and all passed on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest in innet_181026_1634. That baseline ran at spec 3.2.3, and the selector contract is unchanged at 3.2.7 apart from the storyboard's new requires: [controller]. At the pin, update_media_buy splits in two. Package-updates must refuse format_option_refs, format_kind and params, but the immutable-field guard covers only format_ids, so production drops them silently. new_packages must honour the package-request contract, but src never reads new_packages. No scenario grades either update case. Under the owner's decision the canonical_formats storyboard grades nothing here, so the BDD feature is the only grader. Status: PARTIAL.

Code

  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:923 selector_routes(): builds one route per arm (format_option_refs, format_kind+params, format_ids) in the pin's precedence order. params attach only to format_kind.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:1107 resolve_routes(): resolves every route independently against product format_options[]. SelectorResolution.unresolved / conflicting / unsatisfied / selected implement pin steps (1)-(4).
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:1206 canonical_refusal(): raises UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE with rejected_value/accepted_values.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:1265 conflicting_selectors(): raises CONFLICTING_SELECTORS with field=packages[i] and a selector_routes summary for each route.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:753 _resolve_package_selectors(): passes pkg.format_kind, pkg.format_option_refs, pkg.params and pkg.format_ids to resolve_routes and raises CONFLICTING_SELECTORS or UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE with an arm-level field path.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:3174 Called once before both approval branches, so auto-approval and manual-approval tenants are held to the selector.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:3729 The winning route's selected declaration is recorded as the package formats_to_provide snapshot (_package_format_snapshots at line 214).
  • src/core/schemas/_base.py:2884 Package.echoing(): echoes the buyer's format_option_refs / format_kind / params onto the response package (core/package.json SHOULD echo).
  • src/core/schemas/_base.py:3302 AdCPPackageUpdate._IMMUTABLE_PACKAGE_FIELDS = {product_id, format_ids, pricing_option_id}. It omits format_option_refs, format_kind and params, which the pinned package-update `not` also forbids. Probed: in dev they get a generic extra_forbidden refusal, not the readOnly/INVALID_REQUEST refusal that format_ids gets. Under production extra=ignore they would be silently dropped.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_update.py:495 The update path only uses satisfies() for creative assignment against stored package selectors. Nothing under src/ references new_packages (git grep returns nothing), so selectors on update_media_buy new_packages[] are never resolved.

§ Basic structure: "- `format_kind` names the canonical creative shape. ... Compound `format_id` references are deprecated in 3.2. New manifests use the canonical branch." § Exact option routing: "When a product accepts multiple declarations of the same kind, or when the creative must target an exact publisher format, add `format_option_ref`." § Deprecated compatibility branch: "Older 3.x peers may still send a manifest with a compound named `format_id`. A 3.2 implementation may read it, normalize it to a canonical declaration, and project it back when negotiating with that peer. It must not author new manifests from the legacy shape or mix `format_id` and `format_kind` in one manifest." Schema core/creative-manifest.json description: "In 3.2 a manifest uses `format_kind` (the canonical format name it targets), paired with optional `format_option_ref` when a product has multiple declarations sharing that kind. The deprecated `format_id` named-format branch remains only for 3.x wire compatibility." Root oneOf: canonical arm `required: ["format_kind"], not: {required: ["format_id"]}`; the named arm is `deprecated: true`.

M2 IMPLEMENTED format_option_ref is required when the target product carries multiple format_options sharing a format_kind

2026-10-28: ABSENT (grader ABSENT, skeptic ABSENT). 2026-10-29: PARTIAL. 2026-10-30: PARTIAL. Wave 8 (tree ef4d8f5fa): IMPLEMENTED. Wave 11 (tree ff5795e1a): IMPLEMENTED. Graded at 813ee8dff (innet_301026_1301): yes; on the wave 11 tree: pending box run. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.13.

"Required when the target product carries multiple `format_options` entries sharing the same `format_kind`; optional when `format_kind` alone routes the manifest to a single declaration. Product-scoped refs require an enclosing target product/package context." (core/creative-manifest.json format_option_ref.description). Normative restatement, docs/creative/canonical-formats.mdx L1278: "`format_option_ref` is **REQUIRED** on the manifest when the target product's `format_options` contains two or more declarations sharing the same `format_kind` — without it, the seller can't disambiguate which option the buyer is shipping against." Seller side, docs/creative/creative-manifests.mdx § Validation: "1. Resolve `format_option_ref` when present; otherwise select the unambiguous compatible `format_kind` declaration. ... 5. Reject unknown or uncovered selections; do not silently relax the target contract."

static/schemas/source/core/creative-manifest.json#/properties/format_option_ref/description @ v3.2.7; docs/creative/canonical-formats.mdx § "Worked example — multi-format product" (Routing rule for multi-element format_options, L1276-1279) @ v3.2.7; docs/creative/creative-manifests.mdx § Exact option routing and § Validation (L40-118) @ v3.2.7. Since the previous audit: unchanged. The schema description and the creative-manifests.mdx § Exact option routing text the audit quoted are the same at v3.2.7. canonical-formats.mdx L1278-1279 states the same rule as a normative routing rule, and creative-manifests.mdx § Validation gives the seller-side corollary: resolve the ref, otherwise pick the one unambiguous declaration, otherwise reject.

Wave 11, read at ff5795e1a

Owner decision: strict, as built. format_option_ref is required whenever the target product's format_options repeats the manifest's format_kind, whether the manifest fits one of those declarations or several. That is the normative "Routing rule for multi-element format_options" in canonical-formats.mdx. Because creative-manifests.mdx § Validation step 1 can be read as filtering by fit first, the wording question is filed upstream as adcp#8625, asking for explicit text. No repository work follows from it unless upstream adopts the fit-filtered reading.

Graded: the six M2 scenarios now cite the pinned text exactly: canonical-formats.mdx's routing rule, creative-manifests.mdx § Exact option routing and § Validation, product-format-declaration.json format_option_id, and enums/error-code.json (4195044e6; comments only, so the nodeids are unchanged). All six passed on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest in innet_301026_1301 at 813ee8dff.

Residual: adcp#8625; no conformance storyboard step grades manifest routing (ungraded upstream; the issue suggests one).

Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.13 can close.

The wave 8 grade (tree ef4d8f5fa)

Wave 8, graded at ef4d8f5fa

Built: the strict reading (71f7858ad). canonical-formats.mdx's routing rule, marked normative, makes format_option_ref REQUIRED when the product holds two or more declarations of the manifest's kind, so a ref-less manifest is refused MANIFEST_AMBIGUOUS whether it fits one of them or several. A placement that narrows one product option into two entries does not make the ref required (_fills).

Graded: @T-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION-manifest-ref-required and its ref-naming accept twin, -manifest-ambiguous, -manifest-ambiguous-resolved-by-ref, and -creatives-cover-placement-narrowing-one-option-twice / -creatives-miss-placement-narrowing-one-option-twice (09720d303). Every two_image_options creative scenario now names its option, and at the merge so do F3's two publisher-slice creative scenarios. Not yet run.

Residual: the owner must confirm the strict reading, which the 2026-10-30 grade put to them; and a box run on all four transports.

The REQUIRED clause is graded inversely: rows two_image_options|image_300x250, two_image_options|image_728x90 and two_mrec_options|image_300x250_gif of @...-manifest-sync-accepted (BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature:1992) pass on all four transports and assert that a ref-less manifest is accepted against a product with two same-kind declarations.

Evidence

Pin v3.2.7: core/creative-asset.json and core/creative-manifest.json /properties/format_option_ref description: "Required when the target product has multiple format_options entries sharing the same format_kind; optional when format_kind alone routes the creative to a single declaration". docs/creative/canonical-formats.mdx:1278, routing rule marked normative: "format_option_ref is **REQUIRED** on the manifest when the target product's format_options contains two or more declarations sharing the same format_kind". docs/creative/creative-manifests.mdx § Validation step 1: "Resolve format_option_ref when present; otherwise select the unambiguous compatible format_kind declaration." Code: src/core/format_satisfaction.py:1346-1435 (_kind_route). It refuses MANIFEST_AMBIGUOUS only when the manifest satisfies two or more candidates (1427-1435). When exactly one is compatible it returns that selection (1425-1426), so a ref-less manifest is accepted against a product with two declarations of its kind.

Gaps

Production accepts a ref-less manifest whose assets fit exactly one of the product's two or more same-kind declarations. Two pin texts say the ref is REQUIRED in that case: the schema description and the normative routing rule in canonical-formats.mdx. The feature file reads creative-manifests.mdx § Validation step 1 ("select the unambiguous compatible format_kind declaration") as permitting this. The pin conflicts with itself here, so this needs an owner decision; it is not an error-code question. If the owner adopts the strict reading, _kind_route at :1425 must refuse when len(candidates) > 1 and no ref is present (CREATIVE_VALIDATION_FAILED_GENERIC, the code manifest-ambiguous already uses). The three accept rows of @...-manifest-sync-accepted would then move to refusal rows with an @source citation of canonical-formats.mdx L1278. If the owner adopts the step-1 reading, M2 is IMPLEMENTED with no code change, and the RFC row should record that reading.

Beads

  • salesagent-ol033.20.13 (update notes): The ABSENT title is stale. The several-compatible refusal and ref resolution are built and graded on all four transports (manifest-ambiguous, manifest-ambiguous-resolved-by-ref). What remains is an owner decision on the single-compatible case (format_satisfaction.py:1425-1426), where the schema and canonical-formats.mdx:1278 conflict with creative-manifests.mdx § Validation step 1. The notes should name the three manifest-sync-accepted rows that flip under the strict reading. Keep open until that decision.
The 2026-10-29 grade (tree 1b5c6880b, outcomes from innet_291026_0257)

Partly built. A manifest without format_option_ref that fits two or more declarations of its kind is refused CREATIVE_VALIDATION_FAILED_GENERIC with the compatible options named, and naming one of them resolves it. A ref-less manifest whose assets fit only one of the product's same-kind declarations is still accepted and assigned to that declaration (src/core/format_satisfaction.py:1409). The pin does not allow that: core/creative-asset.json and core/creative-manifest.json say format_option_ref is "Required when the target product has multiple `format_options` entries sharing the same `format_kind`", and canonical-formats.mdx's routing rule (normative) says it "is **REQUIRED** on the manifest when the target product's `format_options` contains two or more declarations sharing the same `format_kind`". creative-manifests.mdx § Validation step 1 ("otherwise select the unambiguous compatible `format_kind` declaration") covers the case where the kind alone routes to a single declaration, and step 5 says "do not silently relax the target contract". So the seller should refuse every ref-less manifest assigned to a product with two or more declarations of its kind.

The obligation still holds at v3.2.7 with the same wording, and it applies to this seller, which checks creative manifests against a product's format_options[] at sync_creatives assignment and at create/update_media_buy. Production resolves a format_option_ref when one is present. When it is absent, _kind_route (format_satisfaction.py:779-784) treats every same-kind declaration as a candidate. For a stored creative the params are None, so grading is skipped and the first declaration is silently accepted. Nothing counts same-kind options, nothing requires the ref, and nothing refuses an ambiguous manifest, so the status is ABSENT. No BDD scenario grades the rule. The 'two_image_options' rows in BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature are labelled M2 and pass on all four transports, but they test the package selector (format_kind + params), which the pin allows to resolve by directional satisfaction. The UC-018 format_option_ref scenario tests only the stored round-trip. Not graded on any transport. The canonical_formats storyboard does not count, because comply_test_controller is not part of this seller.

Code

  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:803 _canonical_lane: when the stored creative carries an option it routes through _option_route (exact match on FormatOptionIdentity). The ref is resolved when present (Validation step 1, first half).
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:807 _canonical_lane: with no option, a bare format_kind goes to _kind_route. Nothing counts how many declarations share that kind, and nothing requires the ref when two or more do.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:779 _kind_route grades every same-kind declaration as a candidate ...
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:784 ... and returns the first satisfied candidate, otherwise candidates[0]. Nothing checks for ambiguity and no refusal for a missing format_option_ref exists.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:678 _graded: params None (every stored creative, per the satisfies() docstring) skips grading, so the first same-kind declaration is always 'satisfied'. A ref-less manifest against a product with two image options silently binds to whichever is declared first. Assets are never compared, so the 'unambiguous compatible' selection the pin allows is not made either.
  • src/core/tools/creatives/_assignments.py:262 sync_creatives assignment gate calls satisfies(stored_identity.as_selector(), product.format_options, ...). It refuses only when the verdict is falsy, which never happens for the ambiguous ref-less case.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:4239 create_media_buy creative gate: the same satisfies() call, with the same silent acceptance.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_update.py:495 update_media_buy creative gate: the same satisfies() call, with the same silent acceptance.
  • src/core/database/models.py:1036 creatives.format_option_ref column exists, so a ref the buyer sends is stored. Its absence is never checked against the product.

The pin names no error code for this case. A scenario would need the refusal code decided first, most likely UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE or VALIDATION_ERROR, by spec reconciliation.

This matters less, because a legacy format_id creative is outside the canonical format_option_ref arm, but it is the same missing disambiguation.

M3 IMPLEMENTED Validate the manifest against the selected declaration : resolve the ref, confirm required slots, validate each asset's type and technical constraints, check fixed dimensions/durations/codecs/file sizes, reject uncovered selections without relaxing the target. creative-manifests.mdx § Validation, steps 1–5

2026-10-28: PARTIAL (grader PARTIAL, skeptic PARTIAL). 2026-10-29: PARTIAL. 2026-10-30: PARTIAL. Wave 8 (tree ef4d8f5fa): PARTIAL. Wave 11 (tree ff5795e1a): IMPLEMENTED. Graded at 813ee8dff (innet_301026_1301): partly; on the wave 11 tree: pending box run. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.14.

Validate a manifest against the selected product or publisher declaration: 1. Resolve `format_option_ref` when present; otherwise select the unambiguous compatible `format_kind` declaration. 2. Confirm all required slots are present. 3. Validate each asset against its declared type and technical constraints. 4. Check fixed dimensions, durations, codecs, file-size limits, and required disclosures. 5. Reject unknown or uncovered selections; do not silently relax the target contract. Creative agents can advertise `validate` in `creative.supported_formats[].operations`. Sellers perform final acceptance during `sync_creatives` because product-, account-, and placement-specific constraints remain seller-authoritative.

Production is PARTIAL. At sync_creatives, a canonical manifest gets only the format_kind vocabulary check (_validation.py:141-148) and then returns (lines 154-164). When a creative is assigned to a package (at sync, create or update), satisfies() runs steps 1 and 5 at kind/option granularity: uncovered kinds or options are refused with UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE in both validation modes. The substance of the obligation, steps 2-4, is not implemented on any path: required slots present, asset types and technical constraints, fixed dimensions, durations, codecs, file sizes and disclosures. format_satisfaction.py:678 skips params grading for every creative, and _slot_shortfall applies only to package selectors (manifest-side grading is the unbuilt salesagent-ol033.7). The legacy arm has a TODO(#767) at _validation.py:203 for the same gap. The BDD scenarios that come closest (BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION sync-refused/accepted/lenient, creative-refused/accepted, update-refused, creative-asset-unnamed) pass on all four transports and assert wire codes. They grade only the kind closed set or a trafficking URL lookup. The sized and density scenarios grade legacy format_id reference parameters, not manifest assets. No scenario validates a manifest's slots, asset types or dimensions against the selected canonical declaration, so M3 is ungraded. The controller-gated storyboards grade nothing on this seller.

Code

  • src/core/tools/creatives/_validation.py:141 Canonical arm at sync: the only check is that format_kind belongs to the pinned CanonicalFormatKindStr vocabulary. An out-of-vocabulary kind is refused with VALIDATION_ERROR on field format_kind (lines 141-148).
  • src/core/tools/creatives/_validation.py:154 With no format_id assertion the function returns validated_creative at line 164. The comment says 'the vocabulary check above is the whole of its validation'. So at sync there is no format_option_ref resolution, no required-slot check, no asset type or constraint check, and no dimension, duration, codec, file-size or disclosure check (steps 1-4 absent).
  • src/core/tools/creatives/_validation.py:203 Legacy arm: the only check is that the creative agent serves the format id (fetch_format_spec; REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND otherwise). Line 203 reads 'TODO(#767): Call validate_creative ... to validate that creative manifest matches format requirements'. No manifest-against-format validation on this arm either.
  • src/core/tools/creatives/_sync.py:217 The only call site of _validate_creative_input on the sync path.
  • src/core/tools/creatives/_assignments.py:262 When a sync carries assignments, satisfies() checks the stored creative identity (kind / format_option_ref) against the product's format_options[]. An uncovered kind or option is refused with UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE in both validation modes (line 280). This covers step 1 (option-ref routing / kind selection) and step 5 (reject uncovered), at kind/option granularity only.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:678 _graded: when params is None, grading is skipped. Every stored creative passes params=None (module docstring lines 44-49; satisfies() docstring at line 1171). A manifest's assets are therefore never graded against the declaration's fixed dimensions or durations. Deferred to salesagent-ol033.7.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:330 _slot_shortfall implements 'required slots present with compatible asset types' only for PACKAGE SELECTORS. Its docstring says manifest-side grading (required slots against a creative's actual assets) is salesagent-ol033.7 and unimplemented.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:819 _legacy_lane compares the creative's legacy format_id PARAMETERS (width/height/duration/pixel_ratio) with the product's format_ids. These are the reference's parameters, not the manifest's asset values, and they are on the retained legacy arm.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:616 The create_media_buy pre-adapter gate reads the media URL and dimensions through the declaration's slot ids. It refuses only when the URL is missing or a sized slot has no dimensions (lines 618-632). It does not compare the dimensions with the declaration's fixed values, and it runs at create_media_buy, not at sync acceptance.

M4 IMPLEMENTED A manifest must not mix format_id and format_kind (root oneOf , each arm with a not ). creative-manifests.mdx § Deprecated compatibility branch; core/creative-asset.json , core/creative-manifest.json

2026-10-28: PARTIAL (grader PARTIAL, skeptic PARTIAL). 2026-10-29: IMPLEMENTED. 2026-10-30: IMPLEMENTED. Graded on all four transports: yes. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.15.

creative-manifests.mdx § Deprecated compatibility branch: "Older 3.x peers may still send a manifest with a compound named `format_id`. A 3.2 implementation may read it, normalize it to a canonical declaration, and project it back when negotiating with that peer. It must not author new manifests from the legacy shape or mix `format_id` and `format_kind` in one manifest." core/creative-asset.json root: "oneOf": [{"title": "v1 creative (named-format reference)", "deprecated": true, "required": ["format_id"], "not": {"required": ["format_kind"]}}, {"title": "v2 creative (canonical format kind)", "required": ["format_kind"], "not": {"required": ["format_id"]}}]. core/creative-manifest.json carries the same root oneOf ("Named-format manifest" deprecated / "Canonical-format manifest").

docs/creative/creative-manifests.mdx § Deprecated compatibility branch (line 130-132 at v3.2.7); static/schemas/source/core/creative-asset.json#/oneOf; static/schemas/source/core/creative-manifest.json#/oneOf. Since the previous audit: Unchanged in substance. At v3.2.7 the mdx sentence also bans authoring new manifests from the legacy shape, and both creative-asset.json and creative-manifest.json carry the root oneOf with a `not` in each arm. The format_id arm is marked deprecated: true. The seller receives creative-asset.json on sync_creatives creatives[] (sync-creatives-request.json:49), create_media_buy packages[].creatives[] (package-request.json:313) and update_media_buy packages[].creatives[] (package-update.json:205). list-creatives-response.json items carry the same exclusive oneOf for what the seller emits.

Production status is PARTIAL: - sync_creatives and create_media_buy refuse a mixed item at the boundary via CreativeAssetRequest._exactly_one_format_identifier (creative.py:197-240, [type=oneOf]). - update_media_buy's AdCPPackageUpdate keeps the SDK's CreativeAsset, which only checks 'at least one', so a mix passes the boundary. Code

  • src/core/schemas/creative.py:197 CreativeAssetRequest._exactly_one_format_identifier is a mode=before model_validator. It counts format_id/format_kind that are `is not None` (lines 235-238) and raises PydanticCustomError('oneOf', 'provide exactly one of format_id or format_kind') at line 240 when the count is not 1. Probe: both -> REJECT [type=oneOf]; neither -> REJECT [type=oneOf].
  • src/core/schemas/creative.py:236 The count uses `is not None`, so {format_kind: 'image', format_id: null} counts as one and is ACCEPTED (probe confirmed). At the pin, `required` counts presence, so this payload matches neither oneOf arm and is invalid. The SDK's own _require_schema_required_group treats an explicit null as supplied, so the local check diverges from the SDK too.
  • src/core/schemas/creative.py:518 SyncCreativesRequest.creatives is list[CreativeAssetRequest], so sync_creatives refuses a mixed item at the boundary.
  • src/core/schemas/_base.py:2795 PackageRequest.creatives is list['CreativeAssetRequest'], rebuilt at src/core/schemas/__init__.py:27, so create_media_buy inline creatives refuse a mix at the boundary.
  • src/core/schemas/_base.py:3272 AdCPPackageUpdate(LibraryPackageUpdate) does not redeclare creatives. The field stays the SDK's list[adcp...creative_asset.CreativeAsset], which checks only 'at least one' (_require_schema_required_group). Probe: AdCPPackageUpdate.model_validate with both format_kind and format_id in creatives[0] is ACCEPTED at the boundary.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_update.py:1174 The update path rebuilds SyncCreativesRequest(creatives=pkg_update.creatives, ...) inside the impl. Because of from_attributes the validator runs again and refuses the mix there. Probe: errors [('oneOf', ('creatives', 0))]. The refusal happens late, inside the update loop, and its loc is /creatives/0 rather than the buyer's /packages/N/creatives/M.

Code

  • src/core/schemas/_base.py:584 FormatOptionIdentity.document now writes the 'scope' discriminator and leaves out publisher_domain on the product arm. This fixes the audit's union_tag_not_found readback break.

  • src/core/creative_agent_registry.py:992 The outbound preview_creative request carries only format_id as a FormatId object plus the caller's manifest. format_kind and format_option_ref never reach the creative agent.

M6 IMPLEMENTED Carry representation_selection when the manifest was resolved from equivalent trafficking representations. creative-manifests.mdx § Libraries and trafficking

2026-10-28: ABSENT (grader ABSENT, skeptic ABSENT). 2026-10-29: ABSENT. 2026-10-30: IMPLEMENTED. Graded on all four transports: yes. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.17.

"When a manifest was resolved from a set of equivalent trafficking representations, it carries `representation_selection`. That object preserves the durable `creative_id`, complete source `revision_id` plus digest, selected `representation_id`, applied deterministic strategy, and derived-output digest used for review. It is not a rotation or served-variant instruction." (creative-manifests.mdx:128). The seller-side normative clauses that bind a sales agent: sync_creatives.mdx:231-244 "When an item carries `representation_selection`, its `creative_id` and `revision_id` must equal the selection lineage. The seller binds revision identity to `representation_selection.revision_content_digest` ... The seller separately verifies `selected_output_digest` ... [`list_creatives`] returns the exact current selection lineage while that execution remains current." Also list-creatives-response.json:85-97 representation_lineage_readback {"required_when_current_creative_was_resolved_from_representation_set": true, "must_equal": "last_accepted_current_representation_selection"}.

docs/creative/creative-manifests.mdx § Libraries and trafficking (line 128) @ v3.2.7; seller obligations at docs/creative/task-reference/sync_creatives.mdx lines 231-266, docs/creative/representation-sets.mdx § Sync, review, and readback, static/schemas/source/creative/sync-creatives-request.json (representation_set_projection, lines 95-106; localization exclusion lines 137-149), static/schemas/source/creative/list-creatives-response.json#/…/representation_selection (lines 85-97). Since the previous audit: The obligation sentence is the same. The audit cited the test vector at an old-pin bundle path (_compliance/3.2.1/test-vectors/...). At v3.2.7 the vector is at static/compliance/source/test-vectors/creative-representation-resolution.json, and no storyboard under static/compliance/source references representation_selection, so nothing in the pin's compliance suite grades it. At v3.2.7 the seller-side duties are spelled out as verifier constraints: on sync, the identity must match, the revision binds to revision_content_digest, and selected_output_digest is verified. Readback on list_creatives must return the selection. The two cannot be mixed with localization. The build_creative resolver mode is opt-in through creative.representation_resolution.

That storyboard has requires: [controller], and this seller has no comply_test_controller, so it grades nothing here.
  • Incorrect scenario: T-UC-010-features-partitions (BR-UC-010-discover-seller-capabilities.feature:759). The rows mixed_typical, all_true, all_false and with_additional_properties are unwired: they are missing from _SATISFY_TABLE at uc010_capabilities.py:1638-1654 and raise NotImplementedError (#1592). The Given at uc010_capabilities.py:543 writes no tenant input for the flag. The all_false row describes a state production cannot produce while it accepts inline creatives. The rows treat the flag as free configuration, not as a commitment tied to acceptance.
  • Unrealised scenario on all four transports: T-UC-002-alt-creatives (BR-UC-002-create-media-buy.feature:110) xfails at setup ('UC-002 harness not yet wired'). Its inline-creatives Given and its upload/assign Thens have no step definitions. The persistence-gap FIXME at tests/bdd/conftest.py:734-739 has never been measured.
  • Unrealised scenario on all four transports: T-UC-003-alt-creatives-inline (BR-UC-003-update-media-buy.feature:134) xfails at setup ('UC-003 harness not yet wired'). The strict FK-violation xfail at tests/bdd/conftest.py:2192-2203 has never been measured. Its Thens (status completed, affected_packages) do not check that the inline creative was assigned or read back.
  • Production (minor): src/services/seller_capabilities.py:634 justifies the flag by sync_creatives/list_creatives, which is the spec's meaning of creative.has_creative_library. The flag is hardcoded True at :635 rather than derived from the create/update code paths (media_buy_create.py:3101, media_buy_update.py:1155) that justify it. The value is correct today, but nothing keeps the flag and the behavior in step.
  • Risk, unverified: the two conftest FIXMEs (create: inline creatives not persisted; update: FK violation in the assignment path) would each make the declared true a broken promise under 'If a seller declares a feature as true, they MUST honor requests using that feature.' The baseline cannot confirm or refute them because the scenarios never executed. The status IMPLEMENTED rests on reading the code plus three passing error-path scenarios that reach the inline path, not on any passing scenario where inline creatives are accepted.
  • Update does not replace. At v3.2.7, package-update.json /properties/creatives says 'Replace this package's inline creative assets', update_media_buy.mdx:354 says 'Replace assigned inline creative bodies for this package', and update_media_buy.mdx:1029 says the array 'replaces the package's inline creative bodies' (add, replace and removal workflows). The inline path at media_buy_update.py:1155-1219 only syncs the new creatives and upserts Assignment rows through sync_creatives/_process_assignments (creatives/_assignments.py:89, documented as an 'idempotent upsert'). It never deletes the package's previously assigned creatives. The sibling creative_ids path does delete them (media_buy_update.py ~1113-1123, removed_ids -> uow.assignments.delete_row). After an inline replace, the old creative stays assigned, and get_media_buys creative_approvals (media_buy_list.py:207, :345) would report both. Under the schema's 'If a seller declares a feature as true, they MUST honor requests using that feature', this is a behavioural shortfall behind the declared true. I found it by reading the code. No scenario grades it, and the T-UC-003-alt-creatives-inline Thens would not catch it.
  • This seller declares inline_creative_management=true and never emits creative.has_creative_library (grep over src/ finds no hits). To a buyer it is therefore an inline-only seller, and at the pin get_media_buys creative_approvals is the only readback surface for inline creatives (get_media_buys.mdx:168). No scenario reads creative_approvals back after an inline create or update.
  • The one passing positive create row (T-UC-002-boundary-creative-asset 'valid inline upload') builds its inline creative with a legacy format_id, not format_kind. It checks only that a media_buy_id came back, so it cannot confirm or refute the 'inline creatives not persisted' FIXME at conftest.py:734-739.

C3 N/A Declare creative.supports_revisions and preserve immutable buyer revision_id values through sync, review, readback and delivery. sales-agent-creative-capabilities.mdx § Creative management modes

2026-10-28: ABSENT (grader ABSENT, skeptic ABSENT). 2026-10-29: N/A. 2026-10-30: N/A. Graded on all four transports: not applicable. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.20.

| `creative.supports_revisions: true` | The library accepts immutable buyer `revision_id` values and preserves them through sync, review, readback, and delivery. Requires `has_creative_library: true`; does not imply staging or rollback. |

docs/creative/sales-agent-creative-capabilities.mdx § Creative management modes (v3.2.7); normative detail in docs/creative/specification.mdx § Creative revision identity and docs/creative/task-reference/sync_creatives.mdx § Creative revisions; schema static/schemas/source/protocol/get-adcp-capabilities-response.json#/properties/creative/properties/supports_revisions (default false). Since the previous audit: Wording of the capability row is unchanged. At v3.2.7 the obligation is explicitly conditional: supports_revisions defaults to false, and specification.mdx says "Without that capability, a receiver may ignore `revision_id` and the buyer MUST NOT rely on immutability, echo, readback, review correlation, or delivery attribution." The guarantees bind only an agent that advertises the flag (which also requires has_creative_library: true). Spec text names the concrete guarantees: echo on created/updated/unchanged, omission on failed/deleted, CREATIVE_REVISION_CONTENT_MISMATCH on reuse with changed content, list_creatives readback, and get_creative_delivery.variants[].revision_id. The audit row's "Declare creative.supports_revisions" is the RFC's goal, not a spec MUST.

Graded

Not applicable (owner decision 2026-10-29). Absence of the creative block is graded by @T-UC-010-degradation-partitions/creative_absent and @T-UC-010-local-unbacked-protocol, which pass on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest in innet_291026_2041. No scenario names supports_revisions or revision_id.

Evidence

src/core/tools/capabilities.py:50-63 emits no creative block, so supports_revisions takes its schema default of false. src/core/schemas/creative.py:325 the sync item now declares revision_id (accepted, not bound). Pin @ v3.2.7: docs/creative/specification.mdx § Creative revision identity ('Without that capability, a receiver may ignore revision_id'); get-adcp-capabilities-response.json creative.supports_revisions requires has_creative_library: true.

Gaps

None under the owner decision. Accepting and ignoring revision_id is pin-conformant for a receiver that does not advertise supports_revisions.

Beads

  • salesagent-ol033.20.20 (close): Already closed as N/A, and nothing at c20a4c2c1 changes that.
The 2026-10-29 grade (tree 1b5c6880b, outcomes from innet_291026_0257)

Not applicable. Owner decision 2026-10-29: the seller does not claim the creative protocol, so creative.supports_revisions cannot be declared (the creative block is present only when creative is in supported_protocols). The bead is closed.

None found at this tree.

Grader: ABSENT. Skeptic: ABSENT (agrees). C3 is still an obligation at v3.2.7, worded the same as the audit row (sales-agent-creative-capabilities.mdx § Creative management modes), but it is opt-in. supports_revisions defaults to false and requires has_creative_library: true. Without the flag, a receiver may ignore revision_id. This seller declares no creative capability section at all (src/core/tools/capabilities.py:50, src/services/seller_capabilities.py:635), so not honouring revision_id is spec-conformant. Nothing the RFC asks for exists, though. The sync DTO (src/core/schemas/creative.py:139) does not accept revision_id. There is no content binding, no CREATIVE_REVISION_CONTENT_MISMATCH, no echo in sync results (_processing.py:254), no list_creatives readback (creative.py:303) and no get_creative_delivery tool. Status: ABSENT. No BDD scenario grades any part of C3, including the current posture (flag absent or false). Code

  • src/core/tools/capabilities.py:50 GetAdcpCapabilitiesResponse is built with adcp, supported_protocols, specialisms, measurement, experimental_features, media_buy, account, webhook_signing, request_signing, identity and errors. It has no creative= section, so creative.supports_revisions is never emitted and falls back to the schema default of false. has_creative_library, which the flag requires, is not emitted either.
  • src/services/seller_capabilities.py:635 The only creative-management declaration is media_buy.features.inline_creative_management=True. No creative.has_creative_library or creative.supports_revisions is derived anywhere in describe_seller.
  • src/core/schemas/creative.py:139 CreativeAssetRequest (the sync_creatives item DTO) extends core CreativeAsset, which has no revision_id field. The pinned sync-creatives-request.json item is allOf[creative-asset, {revision_id, localization}], available in the SDK as SyncCreativesCreative, but the DTO does not use it. A buyer-sent revision_id is stripped (production) or rejected as undeclared (dev, under CLAUDE.md pattern #7). It is never stored.
  • src/core/tools/creatives/_processing.py:254 SyncCreativeResult for updated/unchanged is built from creative_id, action, internal_status and changes only. SyncCreativeResult inherits revision_id from the library model (src/core/schemas/creative.py:549) but nothing sets it, so it is never echoed.
  • src/core/schemas/creative.py:303 Creative (the list_creatives item) inherits revision_id from LibraryCreative, but no code path populates it. A git grep for revision_id, supports_revisions and REVISION_CONTENT_MISMATCH under src/ returns zero matches: no binding of (creative_id, revision_id) to content, no mismatch error, no readback.
  • src/core/tools/registry.py:274 Creative tools registered are list_creative_formats (239), sync_creatives (274) and list_creatives (279). There is no get_creative_delivery, so the delivery-attribution half (variants[].revision_id) has no surface.

E1 IMPLEMENTED Flag divergent dual emission with a non-fatal FORMAT_DECLARATION_DIVERGENT advisory in errors[] , source: "producer" , keeping the get_products response a populated success. canonical_formats storyboard step get_seeded_divergent_product ; enums/error-code.json @ 3.2.1

2026-10-28: IMPLEMENTED (grader IMPLEMENTED, skeptic IMPLEMENTED). 2026-10-29: IMPLEMENTED. 2026-10-30: PARTIAL. Wave 8 (tree ef4d8f5fa): IMPLEMENTED. Graded on all four transports: no. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.21.

enums/error-code.json enumDescriptions.FORMAT_DECLARATION_DIVERGENT: "Non-fatal advisory raised when a product carries BOTH `format_ids` (v1) AND `format_options` (v2) and the two disagree (different canonical, different dimensions, different orientation) after projection. The producer's contract is that both shapes MUST refer to the same underlying declaration; divergence is a producer bug. Either side MAY emit this code: a SELLER may self-detect on emit (own producer bug; rare), or more commonly a consumer-SDK detects on consumption. ... Hard-failing the entire `get_products` response is discouraged — it punishes downstream buyers for the producer bug. ... `error.field` MUST point at the offending product; `error.details` SHOULD carry `{ product_id, format_ids, format_options_summary, divergence_reason }`". The storyboard step get_seeded_divergent_product expects: "Return the seeded canonical_formats_divergent_display product and flag the divergent declarations with a non-fatal FORMAT_DECLARATION_DIVERGENT advisory in `errors[]`", with validations response_schema, products[0].product_id, errors[0].code == "FORMAT_DECLARATION_DIVERGENT", errors[0].source == "producer". canonical-formats.mdx Producer rules: "A producer that cannot guarantee agreement MUST omit the legacy compatibility projection."

static/schemas/source/enums/error-code.json#/enumDescriptions/FORMAT_DECLARATION_DIVERGENT (and #/enumMetadata/FORMAT_DECLARATION_DIVERGENT, recovery "correctable"); static/compliance/source/protocols/media-buy/scenarios/canonical_formats.yaml step get_seeded_divergent_product (lines 700-754, storyboard has requires: [controller] at line 7-8); docs/creative/canonical-formats.mdx § "Deprecated named-format compatibility projection (normative)" (lines 1601-1630). Since the previous audit: Enum description and enumMetadata for FORMAT_DECLARATION_DIVERGENT are byte-identical between v3.2.1 and v3.2.7. The storyboard step's validations are unchanged. What changed is the storyboard header: v3.2.7 adds `requires: [controller]` to canonical_formats.yaml, so the storyboard step no longer grades a seller without comply_test_controller. That includes this seller, so only BDD grades this obligation. For a seller, emitting the code is still a MAY at the pin ("Either side MAY emit"). The obligation is the storyboard's expectation that a self-detecting producer sends it as a populated success with source "producer".

Wave 8, graded at ef4d8f5fa

Built: a v1_format_ref its declaration does not narrow is omitted from the derived format_ids (the producer MUST) and reported as a FORMAT_DECLARATION_DIVERGENT advisory on errors[] of the populated success, source producer, field products[i], details product_id, format_ids, format_options_summary and divergence_reason (ad46ce711). Narrowing is format_satisfaction.declaration_narrows, the same _narrows rule the legacy selector route applies, so a reference the product would not project is one the route would not select. All advisories for the code are built in one place (b8e5a7d59). After the merge that rule reads L4's registry projection.

Three of them assert a divergent dual emission as correct (see gap), so as graders of E1 they are wrong, not vacuous.

Evidence

src/core/format_resolver.py:639-667 CanonicalFormats derives format_ids from the declarations' v1_format_ref through _asserted_refs (:830-869), so the reference axis cannot diverge. src/core/tools/products.py:799-831 emits FORMAT_DECLARATION_DIVERGENT only for platform-extension collisions, which never fire because bundled_extension_definitions is unpopulated (salesagent-hff54). Nothing checks that a declaration's params NARROW its v1_format_ref: _asserted_refs reads the ref and nothing else, and admin authoring (inventory_profiles.py:215 _parse_authored_format_options) does not check agreement. Pin @ v3.2.7: product-format-declaration.json v1_format_ref: 'The v2 declaration's params MUST narrow (be compatible with) each referenced v1 format's requirements'. canonical-formats.mdx:1603-1608: 'When both shapes ship ... they MUST refer to the same underlying format declaration — divergent shapes are a contract violation' and 'A producer that cannot guarantee agreement MUST omit the legacy compatibility projection'. canonical-formats.mdx Narrows § Conflict.

Gaps

Remaining dormant item: the platform-extension collision (salesagent-hff54). The RFC row (002-creative-format-model.md:767, 'implemented | yes') and its line 787 ('met by construction') overstate the status.

The 2026-10-29 grade (tree 1b5c6880b, outcomes from innet_291026_0257)

The reference-axis divergence can no longer occur. A canonical product's format_ids is derived from its declarations' v1_format_ref, so the two arms agree by construction, which is the producer contract the enum states ('both shapes MUST refer to the same underlying declaration'). The seller's advisory for that axis was removed with its details classes, and the scenario that graded it was replaced by one that grades the derived arm. Emitting the code is a MAY for a seller. The other details shape, a platform-extension collision, is still wired in get_products but never fires, because nothing populates the bundled extension definitions.

Code changes since 2026-10-28

  • a60a26c91 fix(products): derive a canonical product's format_ids from its declarations (removes the reference-axis advisory)

IMPLEMENTED and graded on all four transports. At v3.2.7 the FORMAT_DECLARATION_DIVERGENT text is unchanged from 3.2.1. A seller MAY self-detect, and the canonical_formats storyboard step get_seeded_divergent_product expects a populated get_products success carrying the advisory with source "producer". That storyboard now requires comply_test_controller, so it grades nothing for this seller. This seller emits the advisory. announced_legacy_format_arm (src/core/format_resolver.py:723) detects an authored legacy arm that does not contain the declaration's asserted v1_format_ref, omits the product's own divergent arm, and returns pin-shaped details. get_products builds Error.of(FORMAT_DECLARATION_DIVERGENT, field=products[N], source=producer) on a populated success (src/core/tools/products.py:824-832, 871). BDD scenario @T-UC-GET-PRODUCTS-format-options-divergent (feature line 150) asserts the product is served, format_ids is omitted, there is exactly one advisory with source producer, field products[0] and recovery correctable, and the details, all on the wire. It passed on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest in innet_181026_1634. The profile-linked variant also passed on all four. Remaining gaps: divergence is detected only on the reference axis. Arms that disagree on dimensions or kind with no v1_format_ref are neither flagged nor omitted, and no scenario covers that case. The Then step also does not pin the advisory to errors[0].

Code

  • src/core/format_resolver.py:723 announced_legacy_format_arm: decides which legacy arm a product announces and returns the divergence as the advisory's wire details
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:837 no asserted v1_format_ref or no served legacy arm -> no divergence (so divergence is judged only on the reference axis)
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:857 divergence = some asserted v1_format_ref is not in the judged format_ids arm
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:861 for a product's own divergent arm, format_ids=() (legacy projection omitted, per the producer MUST-omit rule). A profile-sourced arm is still served. Details carry product_id, format_ids, format_options_summary, divergence_reason
  • src/core/product_conversion.py:476 conversion records legacy_arm.divergence into the caller's divergences accumulator keyed by product_id
  • src/core/tools/products.py:383 divergences accumulator created in get_products and passed to convert_product_model_to_schema (lines 387, 483)
  • src/core/tools/products.py:826 Error.of(ErrorCode.FORMAT_DECLARATION_DIVERGENT, field=f"products[{index}]", details=..., source=LibraryErrorSource.producer). The index comes from the final response order
  • src/core/tools/products.py:871 GetProductsResponse(products=..., errors=(format_advisories + collision_advisories) or None): advisory on a populated success, not a refusal
  • src/core/errors/details.py:291 FormatDeclarationDivergenceDetails: the pin's details keys plus inventory_profile_id

  • Not a seller gap, recorded for the RFC: the canonical_formats storyboard (requires: [controller]) grades nothing here because comply_test_controller is out of scope for this seller, so BDD is the only grader. Any RFC or audit text claiming 'graded by the canonical_formats storyboard' is stale.

  • The Then steps never assert products[0].product_id directly, which the storyboard's second validation checks. They assert details.product_id == the stored product and field == 'products[0]'. With one product in the tenant the outcome is the same, but the identity of products[0] itself is unpinned.
  • Related producer rule, which belongs to its own obligation and not to E1: canonical-formats.mdx at v3.2.7 says 'Compatibility format_ids MUST be derived from them rather than maintained as a second authored catalog' and 'A producer MAY derive deprecated format_ids ... only for a negotiated older-3.x compatibility response'. This seller keeps format_ids as a second authored column and serves it to every buyer. That is why divergence can arise at all, and it is a separate pin obligation this grade does not cover.
  • Small inconsistency in a docstring: details.py says the details' format_ids is 'the legacy arm the buyer received'. In the product's own-arm case that arm is omitted from the wire (format_resolver.py:861), so the buyer did not receive it. The value is the authored arm, which matches the pin's details key.

E2 IMPLEMENTED Reject disagreeing canonical and legacy selectors, and conflicting canonical routes, with CONFLICTING_SELECTORS ; report an unprojectable legacy route as UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE before attempting equivalence. canonical_formats storyboard steps reject_conflicting_dual_emission , reject_conflicting_canonical_routes , reject_unprojectable_legacy_dual_emission

2026-10-28: PARTIAL (grader IMPLEMENTED, skeptic PARTIAL). 2026-10-29: IMPLEMENTED. 2026-10-30: IMPLEMENTED. Graded on all four transports: yes. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.22.

"Receivers process selectors in this order: (1) independently resolve each present route to canonical declarations, without applying route precedence; an unresolved `format_option_ref` or a legacy `format_id` that cannot be normalized through the canonical mapping path MUST be rejected with `UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE`; (2) when every route resolves, derive the product `format_options[]` entries selected by each route using directional product satisfaction, and require the selected option sets to match; legacy-to-canonical parameter compatibility uses the asymmetric v2 narrows v1 relation, not raw object equality; (3) reject different format shapes, selected option sets, or incompatible dimensions with `CONFLICTING_SELECTORS`; and only then (4) apply precedence: `format_option_refs`, then `format_kind` plus `params`, then `format_ids`. Equivalent legacy dual emission remains valid. A single resolved route that does not satisfy the product is rejected with `UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE`." Also enums/error-code.json CONFLICTING_SELECTORS: "`error.field` SHOULD point at the conflicting package, and `error.details` SHOULD identify the supplied selector routes and their normalized canonical summaries."

static/schemas/source/media-buy/package-request.json#/description (also #/properties/format_ids/description, #/properties/format_option_refs/description, #/properties/format_kind/description); static/schemas/source/enums/error-code.json#/enumDescriptions/CONFLICTING_SELECTORS; docs/protocol/format-references.mdx:70; storyboard static/compliance/source/protocols/media-buy/scenarios/canonical_formats.yaml phase canonical_create_satisfaction, steps reject_conflicting_dual_emission, reject_conflicting_canonical_routes, reject_unprojectable_legacy_dual_emission (all at v3.2.7). Since the previous audit: Wording unchanged in substance: the pin still requires CONFLICTING_SELECTORS for disagreeing canonical/legacy and canonical/canonical routes, and UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE for an unprojectable legacy id before equivalence. The audit's status no longer holds. It said ABSENT, with `git grep CONFLICTING_SELECTORS src/` returning zero and format_option_refs never read. At HEAD both are false: production resolves every route and raises CONFLICTING_SELECTORS. The storyboard steps the audit cited require the controller, so they grade nothing here; the BDD scenarios below do the grading instead.

Graded

Yes. These pass on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest in innet_291026_2041: @T-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION-conflicting (4 rows), -conflicting-option-sets (4), -refused (26; option_plus_unprojectable_legacy and unpublished_option_plus_exact_300x250 = UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE before equivalence), -accepted (19; option_plus_projectable_legacy, direct_plus_legacy and option_plus_equivalent_legacy_image are the negative controls), -legacy-only-option-ref (2), -legacy-only-conflicting (2). The Thens assert the wire code, the package field pointer and the per-route selected-option summaries.

Evidence

Gaps

No production gap against the storyboard's cases. Open interpretation for the owner, row option_plus_broad_legacy (BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature:304): an option ref to the fixed 300x250 option plus the unsized legacy template display_image is graded CONFLICTING_SELECTORS ('format_ids selects nothing'). package-request.json step (2) says 'legacy-to-canonical parameter compatibility uses the asymmetric v2 narrows v1 relation, not raw object equality'. canonical-formats.mdx Narrows § 'Asymmetric narrowing' says a v2 parameter set where v1 says nothing 'is narrowing against the implicit any-value v1 baseline'. Read that way, the fixed option narrows display_image, both routes select the same option, and the request is an equivalent dual emission that must be accepted. The current reading instead relies on product-format-declaration.json ('A broad request with no dimensions or duration does not satisfy a fixed-size ... product'). The pinned storyboard has no broad-legacy case. This needs an owner ruling before the row is treated as correct; if the narrows reading wins, production and the row both change. The legacy_unsized_template single-route refusal raises the same question.

The 2026-10-29 grade (tree 1b5c6880b, outcomes from innet_291026_0257)

Every case the 2026-10-28 grade named is built and graded. An unresolved option ref beside a resolving route is UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE naming format_option_refs. Co-present routes on a legacy-only product are resolved and compared. A broad legacy id beside a fixed option ref selects nothing and is CONFLICTING_SELECTORS. CONFLICTING_SELECTORS details name each route's selected option set, so the verdict no longer depends on declaration order.

E2 is still an obligation at v3.2.7, worded the same, and it applies to this seller as the receiver of create_media_buy. The audit's ABSENT verdict is out of date. Production now resolves every package selector route independently (src/core/format_satisfaction.py:923, 1107). It reports an unprojectable legacy id or an unpublished option ref as UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE before any equivalence check (:1021). It raises CONFLICTING_SELECTORS with field=packages[i] and per-route details when the resolved routes select different declarations (:1035, :1265; src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:815). BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION grades all three storyboard shapes with fixtures that match the pin's sample requests (display_728x90 dual emission, a conflicting direct 728x90 route, and unmapped_legacy_selector_6648). Its Then steps assert the wire code, the recovery, the field pointer and the selector_routes details. Agreeing dual-emission rows act as negative controls, and every row passed on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest in innet_181026_1634. Feature changes since the baseline commit are comment-only. Remaining gaps are narrow: no multi-route scenario with an unresolved option ref, a possibly unhandled conflict or option-ref case on deprecated legacy-only products (not probed), no v2-narrows-v1 broad-legacy acceptance row, and one stale v3.2.3 @source citation.

Code

  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:923 selector_routes builds one FormatRoute per arm (each format_option_refs entry, format_kind+params, each format_ids entry) in the pin's precedence order. A legacy id with no canonical_kind_for projection keeps kind=None instead of being dropped.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:623 _legacy_route returns LEGACY_UNPROJECTABLE when the legacy id neither projects params nor yields a kind (step 1 resolution failure). It returns LEGACY_NOT_SELECTED when it normalizes but selects no declaration.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:1021 SelectorResolution.unresolved reports the first OPTION_NOT_PUBLISHED / LEGACY_UNPROJECTABLE route before any equivalence check (step 1 before step 3).
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:1035 SelectorResolution.conflicting is True only when nothing is unresolved, there are 2+ routes, and the selected declarations differ. A route that selected nothing counts as a differing set.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:1107 resolve_routes resolves every route independently with no precedence. Line 1141 picks _canonical_lane when the product declares format_options, otherwise _legacy_lane.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:1265 conflicting_selectors builds AdCPConflictingSelectorsError with SelectorConflictDetails listing every supplied route (field, selector, normalized format_kind, selected_option).
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:815 _resolve_package_selectors raises conflicting_selectors with field=packages[i] when selector.conflicting. At line 828 it otherwise raises canonical_refusal (UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE) naming the arm of the unresolved/unsatisfied route. It runs for both approval modes before any state is persisted (called at line 3174).
  • src/core/exceptions.py:1410 AdCPConflictingSelectorsError is bound to ErrorCode.CONFLICTING_SELECTORS and typed to SelectorConflictDetails.
  • src/core/errors/codes.py:404 CONFLICTING_SELECTORS maps to HTTP 422 in CODE_TABLE.

Gaps

  • No scenario covers an unresolved format_option_ref beside a second route that resolves (e.g. unpublished option ref plus format_kind image 300x250). Pin step (1) requires UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE naming format_option_refs, not CONFLICTING_SELECTORS. Only the single-route unpublished_option_ref row exists (tests/bdd/features/BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature:111), so the ordering rule is graded for the legacy arm only. Production handles it through OPTION_NOT_PUBLISHED in _RESOLUTION_FAILURES (src/core/format_satisfaction.py:130, 1021).
  • Possible production gap, not probed, on the deprecated legacy-only product branch. When a product declares only format_ids, resolve_routes uses _legacy_lane (src/core/format_satisfaction.py:1141-1142). _legacy_lane returns _SATISFIED with declaration=None for any route that asserts no legacy id (src/core/format_satisfaction.py:864-868). So a format_option_refs route (which cannot resolve against a product with no format_options[]) or a format_kind route alongside format_ids is never resolved or compared, and the package is accepted, not refused with UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE or CONFLICTING_SELECTORS. No scenario covers a legacy-only product with co-present routes.
  • There is no scenario for the asymmetric v2-narrows-v1 compatibility case: a broad legacy id (e.g. display_image with no dimensions) beside a fixed canonical 300x250 route must be accepted as equivalent, not refused. The accept rows only use legacy ids that resolve exactly (display_300x250 via v1_format_ref).
  • Citation drift only, no effect on grading: the feature still cites the storyboard at ref=v3.2.3 path=dist/compliance/3.2.3/protocols/media-buy/scenarios/canonical_formats.yaml (tests/bdd/features/BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature:50), an older bundle, while the other @source lines moved to v3.2.7.
  • The canonical_formats storyboard steps (reject_conflicting_dual_emission, reject_conflicting_canonical_routes, reject_unprojectable_legacy_dual_emission) grade nothing here because the storyboard requires comply_test_controller, which this seller does not include. The obligation is graded only by the BDD outlines above.
  • Confirmed (I ran it, the colleague had only suspected it): on a format_ids-only product, an unresolved format_option_ref is accepted instead of refused with UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE. A format_kind route of a different shape beside format_ids is accepted instead of refused with CONFLICTING_SELECTORS. Source: src/core/format_satisfaction.py:1141-1142, and _legacy_lane's claimed-is-None path at :864-868. No scenario seeds a legacy-only product with co-present routes.
  • The broad legacy id's selection depends on declaration order: production picks the first same-kind declaration without grading params (src/core/format_satisfaction.py:619-627, 793-798). As a result, the v2-narrows-v1 equivalence answers CONFLICTING_SELECTORS or accepts depending on which of two image declarations the option ref names. No scenario covers it.
  • The baseline innet_181026_1634 ran at adcp 9.0.0b3 fork / spec 3.2.3, not at HEAD's adcp 9.3.0 / spec 3.2.7. There is no run of these scenarios at the current pin.

Skeptic's corrections

  • The status should be PARTIAL, not IMPLEMENTED. The colleague's second gap ('possible, not probed') is a confirmed production defect. I called resolve_routes(selector_routes(...)) at HEAD with adcp 9.3.0 / spec 3.2.7 against a product that declares only format_ids (_LEGACY_ARM, no format_options). Three requests were all accepted (bool(resolution)=True, refused=None, conflicting=False): (a) an unpublished format_option_ref alone, (b) the same unpublished ref beside format_ids display_300x250, and (c) format_kind video_hosted beside format_ids display_300x250. The pin's step (1) says 'an unresolved format_option_ref ... MUST be rejected with UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE', and step (3) rejects 'different format shapes' with CONFLICTING_SELECTORS. Cause: resolve_routes picks _legacy_lane whenever the product has no format_options (src/core/format_satisfaction.py:1141-1142). _legacy_lane returns _SATISFIED for any claim that asserts no legacy id, so option-ref and kind routes are never resolved. src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py reads format_option_refs only at line 808, so nothing downstream catches this. A legacy-only product is still legal at the pin: core/product.json at v3.2.7 keeps a deprecated 'Named-format product' branch (required: [format_ids]) and says 'Products MUST carry format_ids, format_options, or both during the 3.x migration window'.
  • The v2-narrows-v1 clause is not just ungraded, it is broken in production, and the result depends on declaration order. Against the two_image_options declaration (image 300x250 'satisfaction_image_mrec', then image 728x90 'satisfaction_image_leaderboard'), production gives different answers for two requests that should get the same one. The leaderboard option ref plus format_ids display_image comes back conflicting=True (CONFLICTING_SELECTORS). The mrec option ref plus the same display_image is accepted. Both canonical options are fixed-size images that narrow the broad v1 display_image, so the pin's 'asymmetric v2 narrows v1 relation, not raw object equality' cannot give different answers for the two. Mechanism: display_image projects no dimensions, so _legacy_route falls back to _kind_route with params=None (src/core/format_satisfaction.py:619-627). _graded skips grading when params is None, so the first same-kind declaration wins (_kind_route at :793-798). The same mechanism means a broad display_image sent alone is accepted against fixed_300x250 (probed: ok=True), while a bare format_kind image is refused (params_not_satisfied). The pin's format_kind description says broad image 'does not satisfy a fixed-size product declaration'.
  • The colleague's claim that only comment lines changed in the feature since the baseline leaves out that the SDK pin moved. The baseline run's meta.json (commit 578c00795) records adcp 9.0.0b3+sa.8qela.f33d4fc71 at spec 3.2.3, while HEAD pins adcp==9.3.0 at spec 3.2.7 (pyproject.toml diff). Production files and step files are unchanged since then, but the behaviour depends on SDK functions (upgrade_legacy_format_id, canonical_kind_for, format_is_supported). I re-probed at HEAD's SDK, and the fixtures still behave as designed: display_728x90 -> image 728x90, display_300x250 -> image 300x250, and unmapped_legacy_selector_6648 -> ValueError, so it is unprojectable. The baseline outcomes therefore plausibly carry over, but the baseline itself was measured at the older pin.

2026-10-28: PARTIAL (grader PARTIAL, skeptic PARTIAL). 2026-10-29: PARTIAL. 2026-10-30: PARTIAL. Wave 8 (tree ef4d8f5fa): IMPLEMENTED. Graded on all four transports: partly. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.23.

"Callers comparing two `format-id` values MUST canonicalize `agent_url` per the AdCP URL canonicalization rules before treating two formats as the same. See docs/reference/url-canonicalization." (core/format-id.json properties.agent_url.description). The rules: "Implementations MUST apply every step and compare the result byte-for-byte." ... "After all eight steps, comparison is byte-for-byte. Implementations MUST NOT apply additional transformations before comparison." (docs/reference/url-canonicalization.mdx)

static/schemas/source/core/format-id.json#/properties/agent_url/description @ v3.2.7; docs/reference/url-canonicalization.mdx § Algorithm (steps 1-8) @ v3.2.7. Since the previous audit: The core/format-id.json text is unchanged: `git diff v3.2.1 v3.2.7` on that file is empty. The file is still marked `deprecated: true` ("DEPRECATED in 3.2") and the MUST still stands. url-canonicalization.mdx gained one pitfall bullet: canonicalization removes only dot segments, never real path segments, so `https://agent.example.com` does not match `https://agent.example.com/mcp`. The algorithm itself is unchanged.

Gaps

  • Production gap: src/core/format_satisfaction.py:865. _legacy_lane decides creative-to-product compatibility (sync_creatives assignments via src/core/tools/creatives/_assignments.py:262, media_buy_update.py:495, and the create gates) through adcp.canonical_formats.format_is_supported. Its canonicalize_agent_url (.venv adcp/canonical_formats/identity.py:17-47) applies only scheme and host lowercasing, userinfo stripping, default-port stripping and empty-path '/'. It skips remove_dot_segments (step 5), percent-encoding normalization (step 6) and trailing-root-dot removal (step 2). Measured: 'https://creative.example.com./api/agent', '.../api/%61gent' and '.../api/x/../agent' each compare unequal to '.../api/agent', while src/core/schemas/_base.py:461 equates all three. The spec says implementations MUST apply every step. The fix is to compare through format_id_identity / canonical_agent_url here, or to fix the SDK upstream.
  • Minor production gap: src/core/creative_agent_registry.py:938. get_format matches on fmt.format_id.id alone inside the dialed agent's catalog and never compares the returned format's agent_url to the requested (canonical) agent_url.
  • Missing scenario: no scenario grades the step-2 IDN and trailing-root-dot rules, step 5 dot segments or step 6 percent-encoding on any surface. This includes the legacy-lane assignment and media-buy compatibility path where the gap above lives, so nothing would turn red there.
  • Missing scenario: no scenario sends a respelling of the same agent to get_products filters.format_ids. The foreign-agent scenario grades pair identity only. Same for the list_creative_formats format_ids filter.
  • Missing scenario: no create_media_buy or update_media_buy scenario sends a packages[].format_ids or creative format_id whose agent_url is a respelling of a registered agent.
  • Weak Then: @T-UC-006-rule-039-inv1's 'the assignment should be created' reads assigned_to from require_payload (tests/bdd/steps/domain/uc006_sync_creatives.py:1864), the reconstructed model, rather than the wire body.
  • Scenario mislabelled: @T-UC-005-inv-049-2-holds (BR-UC-005-discover-creative-formats.feature:177) claims '(agent_url, id) pair' matching but varies only the id, and it is xfailed on e2e_rest (conftest.py:1864).
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:1126-1136 format_accepted_by also delegates to adcp.canonical_formats.format_is_supported, which has the same partial canonicalizer. git grep shows it has no callers in src/, so it is dead code rather than a live violation, but it is a second entry point with the same gap that a future caller would inherit.
  • _legacy_route step 2 (format_satisfaction.py:608, upgrade_legacy_format_id) projects a legacy assertion to canonical params without reading agent_url at all. The pin allows this after projection ('do not exact-match raw (agent_url, id) pairs once a format_id has been projected'), so it is not a canonicalization violation. It does mean a foreign agent's same-id reference can project onto a local declaration. No scenario grades that direction for products with format_options.

L2 IMPLEMENTED Legacy → canonical projection follows the registry's normative order and fails closed; canonical → legacy must not be synthesized. registries/v1-canonical-mapping.json ; RFC #2236 § technical core

2026-10-28: PARTIAL (grader PARTIAL, skeptic PARTIAL). 2026-10-29: PARTIAL. 2026-10-30: IMPLEMENTED. Wave 8 (tree ef4d8f5fa): IMPLEMENTED. Wave 11 (tree ff5795e1a): IMPLEMENTED. Graded at 813ee8dff (innet_301026_1301): partly; on the wave 11 tree: pending box run. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.24.

registries/v1-canonical-mapping.json description: "**Direction of truth (normative).** This registry is authoritative for **v1 → v2 projection only**. v2 → v1 projection MUST rely on `v1_format_ref` on the v2 `ProductFormatDeclaration` — sellers assert the v1 pairing explicitly. SDKs MUST NOT synthesize a v1 `format_id` from the registry by inverting structural matches ... Best-effort inversion is available only from this registry's non-wildcarded literal format_id_glob entries ... SDKs MAY do best-effort inversion for non-wildcarded literals only, AND only when the seller hasn't authored a contradicting `v1_format_ref` — even then, the projection is non-normative and downstream consumers MUST NOT depend on it. **Resolution order** (per RFC #3305 amendment #3767, normative): 1. **Authoritative v2→v1 link** [v1_format_ref] ... 2. **Seller-asserted on the v1 file**: if the v1 format declaration carries an explicit `canonical` field, use it ... 3. **Registry glob** ... 4. **Structural match** ... 5. **Ambiguous family** ... SDKs MUST surface `FORMAT_DECLARATION_V1_AMBIGUOUS` ... 6. **Fail closed**: SDK MUST NOT emit `format_options` for products carrying this format. SDKs MUST augment the response's `errors[]` array with ... `code: "FORMAT_PROJECTION_FAILED"` ... The advisory is non-fatal". Seller-facing form, media-buy/package-request.json description: "(1) independently resolve each present route to canonical declarations ...; an unresolved `format_option_ref` or a legacy `format_id` that cannot be normalized through the canonical mapping path MUST be rejected with `UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE`". docs/creative/canonical-formats.mdx § Format matching vs product satisfaction: "Sellers and SDKs MUST normalize legacy named formats before comparing them to canonical declarations. A legacy `format_id` ... resolves through the explicit `canonical` annotation, `v1_format_ref`, or the canonical mapping registry to a canonical declaration".

static/schemas/source/registries/v1-canonical-mapping.json#/description (Direction of truth; Resolution order steps 1-6) at v3.2.7; static/schemas/source/media-buy/package-request.json#/description (Format selector contract, step 1); docs/creative/canonical-formats.mdx § "Deprecated named-format compatibility projection (normative)" and § "Format matching vs product satisfaction". Since the previous audit: The substance holds at v3.2.7: a six-step order (registry version 1.5.2), v1 to v2 only, and no inversion of structural matches. Three things changed. (a) The registry now has 45 mappings: 38 non-wildcarded literal format_id_glob entries and 7 structural ones. It also adds a MAY for non-normative best-effort inversion of those literals when no v1_format_ref contradicts it. So the RFC line "All seven registry entries published at 3.1 are pure-structural, so nothing inverts even on a best-effort basis" (docs/rfcs/002-creative-format-model.md:102-103) is stale. The prohibition itself is unchanged: inverting structural matches and synthesizing without v1_format_ref are still forbidden, so a seller that never synthesizes stays within the pin. (b) The registry's own fail-closed step (6) is a non-fatal SDK advisory (FORMAT_PROJECTION_FAILED) on product discovery. For a seller, the binding form is package-request.json: an unprojectable legacy id gets UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE. (c) Code changes since the audit: the audit said format_resolver.py:189-200 treats an unresolved projection as "no canonical kind, not a refusal". That is now reversed. creative_identity_of refuses with UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE (format_resolver.py:287-298), and the package route refuses as LEGACY_UNPROJECTABLE (format_satisfaction.py:623). The audit also called the agent's own canonical.kind "step 1"; at the pin that is step 2.

Wave 11, read at ff5795e1a

Built: the owner's wave 11 ruling: a v1 id maps to a kind by the registry glob only at the reference origin, also when a foreign agent serves a declaration with no canonical (77e41f57a). A peer's id projects through its own canonical (step 2) or through the structural entries (step 4), and otherwise fails closed. Step 4 now matches the VAST and DAAST versions three structural entries require (vast >=4.0, vast 3.x/2.x, daast 1.0/1.1) from the slot's vast_version / vast_versions (314207801). Peers that publish ids without canonical see those creatives refused; the owner accepted this because legacy products are obsolete on 2026-11-25.

Graded: @T-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION-upsert-peer-id-skips-glob, -upsert-structural, -upsert-structural-versioned (new), and rows in tests/unit/test_format_resolver.py. Not run. The re-capture moved the refused id of @T-UC-018-format-kinds-filter-matches-the-projected-kind from audio_30s to audio_60s: the reference agent at the pin serves audio_30s with canonical audio_hosted, so step 2 now projects it (0022e116d).

The 2026-10-28 grade (tree 5bf5677fb, outcomes from innet_181026_1634)

Grader: PARTIAL. Skeptic: PARTIAL (disagrees). Applies to this seller: yes (The registry's literal resolution order and its FORMAT_PROJECTION_FAILED/V1_AMBIGUOUS advisories are written for SDKs consuming products from older 3.x peers. This seller produces products and does not consume them, so the advisory surface is not its duty. Two clauses do bind a sales agent. First, canonical-formats.mdx says "Sellers and SDKs MUST normalize legacy named formats ... through the explicit canonical annotation, v1_format_ref, or the canonical mapping registry". Second, package-request.json requires that an unnormalizable legacy format_id be rejected with UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE. The direction-of-truth rule also binds the seller's outbound legacy arms: v2 to v1 relies only on v1_format_ref, and nothing is synthesized from the registry. That covers product format_ids, package format_ids_to_provide and the creative format_id echo.). Graded on all four transports: yes. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.24.

L2 is PARTIAL in production and GRADED on its two main properties. Both properties are still obligations at v3.2.7: legacy resolves to canonical in the normative six-step order and fails closed, and canonical is never synthesized into legacy. For a sales agent they bind through canonical-formats.mdx ('Sellers and SDKs MUST normalize legacy named formats') and package-request.json (unnormalizable legacy format_id gets UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE). They also bind through the registry's direction-of-truth rule, which allows v2 to v1 only through v1_format_ref. The registry's FORMAT_PROJECTION_FAILED / V1_AMBIGUOUS advisories are SDK duties on product consumption and not this seller's. Production implements step 1 (v1_format_ref, package route), step 2 (the agent's explicit canonical) and step 3 (registry glob, plus the SDK upgrade), in the pin's relative order. It fails closed as an UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE refusal on both the package route and the creative write. It never synthesizes legacy from canonical: product format_ids are the authored arm only, format_ids_to_provide is read from v1_format_ref only, and the creative echo is the buyer's assertion only. It does not implement step 4 (structural) or step 5 (ambiguous family), so a structurally projectable format with no canonical annotation is refused instead of projected. Fail-closed and no-synthesis are graded on all four transports in the baseline: refused/option_plus_unprojectable_legacy, upsert-unprojectable/projectable, snapshot-no-legacy-ref and GET-PRODUCTS ref-not-projected all passed on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest. Ungraded: the ORDER between steps (no fixture where two steps disagree), step 1 or step 2 on its own, and the absence of a synthesized format_id on canonically synced creatives. The list_creatives scenario's fail-closed half runs in-process and is close to vacuous on the wire.
