  • src/core/format_resolver.py:151 _glob_canonical: registry step 3, matching v1_pattern.format_id_glob arms only; structural arms (step 4) are skipped by type
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:212 canonical_kind_for tries the fetched spec's explicit canonical.kind (pin step 2) before the glob (step 3); the docstring at :180 calls this 'step 1'
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:231 second glob match after the SDK's upgrade_legacy_format_id (size-encoded slug to template), so the kind is still read off a registry mapping
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:238 raises ValueError when no step resolves (fail closed); steps 4 (structural) and 5 (ambiguous family) are not implemented, as the docstring at :193-198 admits
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:287 creative_identity_of turns an unresolved projection into AdCPCapabilityNotSupportedError (UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE), so the creative is refused rather than stored with a NULL kind
  • src/core/tools/creatives/_processing.py:485 the sync upsert passes the fetched spec so the agent's explicit canonical (step 2) can be reached; the comment at :475 mislabels it 'step 1'

  • src/core/format_resolver.py:733 announced_legacy_format_arm: 'THIS SELLER DOES NOT PROJECT'; product format_ids is only the authored arm, and v1_format_ref is never turned into format_ids

Gaps

  • Production: pin resolution step 4 (structural match against the registry's 7 structural entries: vast, daast, zip, video, audio, url) and step 5 (ambiguous family) are not implemented. canonical_kind_for (src/core/format_resolver.py:238) fails closed instead. A served format with no explicit canonical and no glob hit, but a structurally matchable asset (e.g. a publisher agent's custom hosted-video format), is refused with UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE (format_resolver.py:287; format_satisfaction.py:623) even though the normative order would project it. In the reference-agent capture all projectable formats resolve through steps 2/3, so the gap affects other agents' catalogs only.
  • Production docs: canonical_kind_for's docstring (src/core/format_resolver.py:177-198) and _processing.py:475-476 call the fetched spec's canonical 'step 1'. At the pin it is step 2; step 1 is v1_format_ref. The docstring also says the seller 'does not carry' Product.format_options/v1_format_ref, which is stale: format_satisfaction.py:604 consults v1_format_ref on the package route.
  • Missing scenario: the order between steps is ungraded. No fixture makes two steps disagree, e.g. a fetched spec whose explicit canonical differs from the glob mapping, or a declaration's v1_format_ref pointing at an id the registry upgrade would place on a different declaration. Swapping step 2/3 or step 1/3 in production reddens no scenario (display_300x250 resolves the same through both v1_format_ref and the upgrade).
  • Missing scenario: no scenario covers a legacy id that resolves ONLY through a declaration's v1_format_ref (not in the registry) on the package route, so step 1 alone is never graded.
  • Missing scenario: no scenario covers a creative resolvable only through the agent's explicit canonical (step 2), e.g. a capture format absent from the registry globs. upsert-projectable uses display_300x250_image, which also resolves through the registry upgrade.
  • Missing scenario: no scenario asserts that a creative synced by format_kind is listed or echoed with no synthesized format_id (list_creatives / sync_creatives). Production does not synthesize (mapping/creative.py:235), but nothing grades it.
  • Weak scenario: in @T-UC-018-format-kinds-filter-matches-the-projected-kind (tests/bdd/features/BR-UC-018-list-creatives.feature:1098) the fail-closed half runs in-process in the Given (tests/bdd/test_uc018_list_creatives.py:1988), not on any transport. Its Then (:2109) checks absence from an image-filtered list, which would pass even if the row were stored with another kind.
  • RFC text: docs/rfcs/002-creative-format-model.md:102-103 ('All seven registry entries published at 3.1 are pure-structural, so nothing inverts even on a best-effort basis') is stale at v3.2.7. The registry now has 38 non-wildcarded literal globs and a MAY for non-normative best-effort inversion. The seller's choice never to invert remains compliant.
  • Citation: BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature:50 cites @source ref=v3.2.3 path=dist/compliance/3.2.3/.../canonical_formats.yaml, an older bundle and not the pin. Per the owner decision, that storyboard (requires controller) grades nothing here anyway.
  • Production: the package selector route (create_media_buy/update_media_buy format_ids) skips pin resolution step 2. canonical_kind_for is called without the fetched spec at src/core/format_satisfaction.py:977, so a legacy format_id that resolves only through the creative agent's explicit canonical annotation is refused LEGACY_UNPROJECTABLE / UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE when the product's declaration lacks v1_format_ref. This covers 42 of the 71 formats in the reference capture. sync_creatives accepts and projects the same id, so the two paths are inconsistent. The step-4 gap also matters more than 'other agents' catalogs only' suggests, because on the package route even step 2 is missing.
  • Missing scenario: no package-route scenario uses a legacy id resolvable only through the agent's explicit canonical (for example audio_standard_30s against an audio_hosted declaration without v1_format_ref). Such a scenario would show the step-2 omission above. Every package legacy row (display_300x250, display_728x90, unmapped_legacy_selector_6648) resolves or fails the same way with or without step 2.

Skeptic's corrections

  • Status PARTIAL stands, but one claim in the summary is wrong. It says production implements step 2 (the agent's explicit canonical) 'in the pin's relative order'. That is true only on the creative write path. On the package route, selector_routes calls canonical_kind_for(legacy_ref) with no spec= (src/core/format_satisfaction.py:977). So step 2 is never consulted there, and the legacy route runs step 1 (v1_format_ref, :604) and then step 3 (the registry upgrade at :608 plus the glob kind) only. Probe on HEAD 5bf5677fb: selector_routes(format_ids=[audio_standard_30s]) gives FormatSelector(kind=None), while display_300x250_image gives kind='image'. The fixture tests/fixtures/creative_formats/reference_formats.json declares audio_standard_30s with canonical {'kind': 'audio_hosted'}.
  • Measured on tests/fixtures/creative_formats/reference_formats.json: 22 of the 71 formats resolve without the spec. 42 resolve only through the agent's explicit canonical (step 2), and for all 42 upgrade_legacy_format_id projects no width, height or duration. Examples: audio_standard_15s/30s/60s, broadcast_spot_15s/30s/60s, display_160x600_html and display_160x600_generative. If a package names one of these 42 in format_ids against a canonically declared product whose declaration has no v1_format_ref, it reaches the kind-is-None, no-projection branch and is refused LEGACY_UNPROJECTABLE / UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE (format_satisfaction.py:621-629). The pin's normalization order resolves it (canonical-formats.mdx:1494: 'resolves through the explicit `canonical` annotation, `v1_format_ref`, or the canonical mapping registry'). sync_creatives accepts and projects the same id (_processing.py:485-486 and :1193 pass spec=), so the two write paths disagree about the same reference.
  • Minor: the upsert-unprojectable note describes product_card_standard as '(image+text)'. The fixture also gives it two url assets. Its primary asset is image, so the 'primary asset is a URL' structural entry (display_tag) still does not apply, and the conclusion (unprojectable under all six steps) holds.
  • Confirmed as stated: the pin text and the registry counts at v3.2.7 (version 1.5.2, 45 mappings: 38 format_id_glob and 7 structural); package-request.json step (1); canonical-formats.mdx:1494; the code lines format_resolver.py:151, 212, 231, 238, 287-298, 733 and 992; format_satisfaction.py:604 and 623; mapping/creative.py:235. All nine cited scenarios passed on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest in innet_181026_1634, none is routed to xfail (the conftest rows are env routing only), and their Thens assert wire values. Also confirmed: the stale RFC line at docs/rfcs/002-creative-format-model.md:102-103 and the v3.2.3 @source citation at BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature:50.

(it has no retain or echo clause). docs/creative/creative-manifests.mdx:132: "Older 3.x peers may still send a manifest with a compound named `format_id`. A 3.2 implementation may read it, normalize it to a canonical declaration, and project it back when negotiating with that peer. The obligation therefore comes from the owner decision (RFC 002 Part 4, docs/rfcs/002-creative-format-model.md:292-294: "stored verbatim, echoed back on the legacy wire"), which the pin allows.

The 2026-10-28 grade (tree 5bf5677fb, outcomes from innet_181026_1634)

Grader: IMPLEMENTED. Skeptic: IMPLEMENTED (agrees). Applies to this seller: yes (This seller implements sync_creatives and list_creatives (a sales agent with a creative library), so it decides which arm a listed legacy creative carries. The owner decision says format_id is a retained legacy assertion that is accepted, recorded and echoed, and that decision binds the grade even though the pin only permits the echo.). Graded on all four transports: yes. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.25.

At v3.2.7 the spec does not require echoing the buyer's own format_id verbatim. core/format-id.json never said so, at this pin or any earlier 3.1/3.2 tag, and #2350 is unrelated. The pin only permits a seller to project a legacy reference back (creative-manifests.mdx:132, canonical-formats.mdx:13) and requires each listed creative to carry exactly one of format_id or format_kind (list-creatives-response.json oneOf). The obligation applies here because of the owner decision in RFC 002 Part 4. It is IMPLEMENTED. The buyer's reference is stored as sent in legacy_format_id (mapping/creative.py:131), and agent_url is kept as the raw wire string (verified by execution). It is rebuilt whole by from_row (_base.py:777) and emitted as format_id on list_creatives with format_kind suppressed (_base.py:824, listing.py:523/627). It counts as graded on all four transports through @T-UC-006-storyboard-format-id-roundtrip-on-sync, which passed on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest. That scenario syncs over the wire, reads back with list_creatives and asserts id and agent_url values. Because canonicalisation would add a trailing slash, it catches a seller that stored the canonical form. That grading is narrow: it covers only the trailing-slash respelling, not host case, port or parameters, and it carries a stale v3.2.3 storyboard citation. The two UC-018 scenarios on parameters and arm selection either seed through a factory instead of syncing or assert format_id is not None rather than its value.

Code

  • src/core/database/models.py:1037 legacy_format_id JSONType column holds the buyer's reference as sent; legacy_format_key (:1038, indexed at :1072) holds the canonical comparison key. The comment at :1019-1024 states the verbatim/canonical split.
  • src/core/database/mapping/creative.py:131 identity_columns (:108) writes legacy_format_id = identity.assertion.model_dump(exclude_none=True). This is the one writer of the four identity columns.
  • src/core/schemas/_base.py:755 CreativeFormatIdentity.of_legacy sets assertion=format_id (verbatim, compare=False), and sets legacy/legacy_parameters as the canonical comparison form.
  • src/core/schemas/_base.py:777 from_row rebuilds the assertion with FormatId.model_validate(row.legacy_format_id), which reads the whole stored document, so pixel_ratio is no longer dropped.
  • src/core/schemas/_base.py:824 wire_format_kind returns None whenever an assertion is retained. This is where the response oneOf exclusivity is decided (line :841).
  • src/core/schemas/_base.py:2635 FormatId keeps agent_url as the wire string (StrictStr). Executed: 'https://Creative.AdContextProtocol.org:443/' and the bare origin both survive model_dump unchanged, so there is no trailing-slash or case normalisation.
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:244 creative_identity_of: a legacy reference with no canonical projection is refused with UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE (:285-293) and never retained. Echo applies only to projectable ids, per the owner ruling that format_kind is the identifier.
  • src/core/tools/creatives/listing.py:523 list_creatives reads format_obj = CreativeFormatIdentity.from_row(row).assertion, then emits format_id=format_obj and format_kind=stored_identity.wire_format_kind (:627-628).
  • src/core/tools/creatives/listing.py:270 _ALWAYS_ON_THE_WIRE includes format_id, so a `fields` projection never strips the legacy arm.
  • src/core/database/mapping/creative.py:235 to_domain gives format_id=identity.assertion and format_kind=identity.wire_format_kind, so the domain object carries the arm the buyer sent.

Gaps

  • Citation gap: the obligation row cites core/format-id.json and #2350. Neither states the obligation at v3.2.7 (or at any v3.1.0-v3.2.3 tag). The pin only permits the echo (docs/creative/creative-manifests.mdx:132 'may ... project it back'; canonical-formats.mdx:13 MAY), and its list-creatives-response oneOf constrains the shape. The obligation's real ground is the owner decision in docs/rfcs/002-creative-format-model.md:292-294, and the row should say so.
  • Text inaccuracy: src/core/database/models.py:1019-1020, tests/bdd/test_uc018_list_creatives.py:1614 and BR-UC-018-list-creatives.feature:1036 say 'sync_creatives owes the buyer its own format_id back'. At v3.2.7, creative/sync-creatives-response.json carries no format_id field, so the echo surface is list_creatives, and it is a seller choice the pin permits, not a pin requirement.
  • Missing scenario: nothing syncs a creative over the wire with a host-case or explicit-default-port agent_url (e.g. 'https://Creative.AdContextProtocol.ORG:443') and asserts list_creatives echoes that exact spelling. @T-UC-006-storyboard-format-id-roundtrip-on-sync (BR-UC-006:1713) covers only the trailing-slash difference. @T-UC-018-format-id-filter-canonicalizes-stored-agent-url (BR-UC-018:1026) seeds via factory and asserts ids only.
  • Missing scenario: nothing syncs a parameterized format_id (width/height/pixel_ratio/duration_ms) through sync_creatives and reads it back. @T-UC-018-parameterized-format-id-roundtrip (BR-UC-018:1137) seeds the row with CreativeFactory despite its 'the buyer synced' wording, and it does not assert agent_url or id.
  • Weak Then: BR-UC-018:1104 'that creative should be listed by format_id, carrying no format_kind' (test_uc018_list_creatives.py:2089) asserts format_id is not None instead of equal to the sent reference.
  • Stale citation on the one scenario that does grade the echo: BR-UC-006:1713's @source cites ref=v3.2.3 static/compliance/source/protocols/creative/index.yaml, a storyboard claim the feature itself marks as withdrawn. The echo half needs a v3.2.7 citation (list-creatives-response.json items oneOf plus creative-manifests.mdx:132) and the owner decision.
  • Adjacent defect, outside L3 but found in the same read-back path (the canonical arm of the same oneOf): src/core/tools/creatives/listing.py:270 _ALWAYS_ON_THE_WIRE keeps format_id but not format_kind. Executed: _project_to_fields(Creative(format_kind='image', ...), ['assets']) returns format_kind=None and format_id=None, so a canonical creative listed with a `fields` selection that omits format_kind carries neither arm. That violates list_creatives.mdx:63 ('exactly one of format_id or format_kind') and the items oneOf.
  • A re-sync does not update the stored spelling. In src/core/tools/creatives/_processing.py:485-488 the upsert calls set_identity only when incoming_identity != prior.identity. CreativeFormatIdentity.assertion is declared compare=False (_base.py), and I ran this: of_legacy('https://creative.adcontextprotocol.org') == of_legacy('https://Creative.AdContextProtocol.ORG:443') is True. So if a buyer re-syncs an existing creative with a different spelling of the same agent, legacy_format_id keeps the first spelling and list_creatives returns that old string, not the one just sent. 'Verbatim' therefore means 'as first sent', and no scenario covers a re-sync with a different spelling.
  • 'Verbatim' covers only the fields the DTO declares. format-id.json has additionalProperties: true, but deep_strip_to_schema drops undeclared keys, so an extension key on format_id is never stored or echoed. CLAUDE.md pattern #7 makes this deliberate, but L3's 'verbatim' should be read as 'verbatim over the declared fields'. Separately, a numeric parameter comes back as a float (pixel_ratio 2 is stored and echoed as 2.0); in JSON that is the same value.

2026-10-28: PARTIAL (grader PARTIAL, skeptic PARTIAL). 2026-10-29: PARTIAL. 2026-10-30: PARTIAL. Wave 8 (tree ef4d8f5fa): PARTIAL. Wave 11 (tree ff5795e1a): PARTIAL. Graded at 813ee8dff (innet_301026_1301): partly; on the wave 11 tree: pending box run. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.26.

Do not author new template IDs or treat a deprecated `list_creative_formats` response as a source of truth. [Restated normatively in core/product-format-declaration.json description, "Format matching vs satisfaction (normative)": "Legacy named formats MUST be normalized to canonical declarations before comparison; do not exact-match raw `(agent_url, id)` pairs once a `format_id` has been projected through `canonical`, `v1_format_ref`, or the canonical mapping registry."]

docs/creative/template-format-ids.mdx § Compatibility (git -C ~/projects/adcp show v3.2.7:docs/creative/template-format-ids.mdx); also static/schemas/source/core/product-format-declaration.json#/description (§ Format matching vs satisfaction (normative)) and static/schemas/source/creative/list-creative-formats-request.json#/deprecated (deprecated, x-deprecated-in 3.2.0). Since the previous audit: The text is unchanged: the section is identical at tags v3.2.1 and v3.2.7, apart from link paths. The audit's paraphrase dropped a third clause, "or treat a deprecated `list_creative_formats` response as a source of truth", which is graded here. The code has moved a lot since the audit. format_satisfaction.py now normalizes legacy references through v1_format_ref and upgrade_legacy_format_id. creative_identity_of projects to format_kind at the write and refuses what it cannot project. The seller no longer projects format_ids outbound. So the audit's "matching is still done on the legacy key throughout" is no longer true. It is still true at four specific sites, listed under gaps.

Wave 11, read at ff5795e1a

Accepted ceilings: by the owner's wave 11 decision, the two residuals of the wave 8 grade are accepted as they are, because legacy-only products are obsolete on 2026-11-25: (1) a product that declares only the deprecated format_ids is matched through the SDK's format_is_supported, which upgrades only display_WxH ids, so a video_30s selector or filter does not match a product listing video_pre_roll_30s; (2) density: a display_*_image_2x id projects with no pixel_ratios, so it is treated as the 1x id. Both are recorded in RFC 002 Part 10 (1d06a5b93).

Three production readers remain: get_products filters.format_ids (products.py:547-561), list_creatives filters.format_ids (repositories/creative.py:218), and sync_creatives format existence decided by the agent's list_creative_formats (_validation.py:194-210; also media_buy_create.py _validate_creatives_before_adapter_call via fetch_format_spec). Missing scenarios: a filter sending an id equivalent only after normalisation, and a sync of a registry-projectable id the agent's catalogue does not list. Per the feature comment at BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature (display_300x250 'ABSENT from the capture, so the authoring door refuses it'), the suite currently records the REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND behaviour as intended. That comment contradicts the pin clause and should be corrected together with the production fix.

The 2026-10-28 grade (tree 5bf5677fb, outcomes from innet_181026_1634)

Grader: PARTIAL. Skeptic: PARTIAL (agrees). Applies to this seller: yes (This seller receives legacy format_ids from 3.x buyers on create_media_buy packages, sync_creatives creatives, and the get_products and list_creatives filters. It compares them against product declarations and stored creatives. It also authors product and inventory-profile format catalogs and announces them. It reads creative agents' list_creative_formats. All three clauses (normalize-then-match, do not author template ids, do not treat list_creative_formats as truth) apply to a sales agent. None of them is specific to a creative agent.). Graded on all four transports: yes. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.26.

L4 is still an obligation at v3.2.7, word for word: docs/creative/template-format-ids.mdx § Compatibility, restated as a MUST in core/product-format-declaration.json. The audit's paraphrase dropped the clause "do not treat a deprecated list_creative_formats response as a source of truth". It applies to this sales agent. Since the audit, most of the normalization has been built: - creative_identity_of projects legacy ids to format_kind at the sync write and refuses unprojectable ones. - format_satisfaction normalizes package legacy selectors through v1_format_ref and upgrade_legacy_format_id, and grades legacy-only products by format_is_supported over upgraded references. - The seller no longer synthesizes format_ids outbound, and the admin product surface no longer authors them. BDD grades this on all four transports with correct wire assertions, and every one passed in the baseline: - the list_creatives format_kinds-projected-kind scenario - the upsert projectable/unprojectable pair - the legacy_display_728x90 refusal - the option_plus_conflicting_legacy conflict - the deprecated-lane sized/density sync scenarios - the two get_products no-projection scenarios Status is PARTIAL because raw-key matching remains: - create_media_buy re-checks package format_ids against product.format_ids by exact (agent_url, id) key after satisfaction has accepted them (media_buy_create.py:3749). By code reading, that refuses display_300x250_image against a display_300x250 product, and any legacy selector against a canonical-only product. - The deprecated get_products and list_creatives format_ids filters exact-match raw keys. - sync_creatives treats the creative agent's deprecated list_creative_formats as the authority for whether a legacy id exists. - Inventory profiles still author legacy format_id catalogs. None of these are graded. The legacy accept rows all send the literal display_300x250, so a raw-key seller passes them. The canonical-only purchasable outline explicitly omits the legacy selector.

Code

  • src/core/format_resolver.py:288 creative_identity_of projects a legacy format_id to its canonical kind at the write edge (canonical_kind_for: agent's own canonical, then registry glob, then glob of the upgraded id, :218-241) and refuses an unprojectable one with AdCPCapabilityNotSupportedError at :293. This is the 'normalize immediately' half on sync_creatives.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:604 _legacy_route step 1: matches the asserted reference against each declaration's v1_format_ref (seller-asserted projection).
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:608 _legacy_route step 2: upgrade_legacy_format_id normalizes to canonical params, then grades them against declaration params. It returns LEGACY_NOT_SELECTED (:643) when the normalized shape selects nothing. This is canonical-shape matching for packages against format_options products.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:865 _legacy_lane (legacy-only products) uses adcp format_is_supported, which upgrades both sides before comparing, instead of key membership.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:977 selector_routes projects each package format_ids entry through canonical_kind_for before resolution.
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:733 announced_legacy_format_arm: 'THIS SELLER DOES NOT PROJECT'. No format_ids are synthesized from v1_format_ref or the registry on get_products, so no new template ids are authored outbound.
  • src/admin/blueprints/products.py:177 The admin product surface no longer authors format_ids; new products must author format_options.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:3749 GAP: after selector satisfaction has already accepted the package, a second gate builds product_format_identities(pkg_product.format_ids) and product_format_identities(matching_package.format_ids) and refuses the set difference (:3752-3775, AdCPValidationError). This exact-matches raw (agent_url, id) keys. The comment at :823 confirms the deprecated-lane refusal is deliberately left to this branch.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:3781 GAP: the dimension-merge map is keyed on legacy FormatKey, so it is legacy-keyed matching.
  • src/core/tools/products.py:572 GAP: get_products filters.format_ids is matched by product_format_identities(product.format_ids) & requested_identities, which is raw-pair intersection with no normalization.
  • src/core/database/repositories/creative.py:214 GAP: list_creatives filters.format_ids is Creative.legacy_format_key.in_(...), an exact match on the raw legacy key.
  • src/core/tools/creatives/_validation.py:188 GAP (list_creative_formats as truth): a legacy format_id on sync_creatives is refused REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND (:197-202) unless fetch_format_spec finds it. fetch_format_spec calls registry.get_format, which reads get_formats_for_agent, i.e. the agent's deprecated list_creative_formats (creative_agent_registry.py:900-933, :453). The canonical registry projection is not consulted for this decision.
  • src/admin/blueprints/inventory_profiles.py:334 GAP (authoring): inventory profiles still store operator-supplied format_ids parsed from free JSON (:205, :419-421). Linked products serve these via effective_format_ids, so new legacy or template format_id catalogs can still be authored.

V1 N/A The seller's accepted format_kind vocabulary is the seller's, not the library's. adcp-client-python PR #1415 (open, docs); is_canonical_format_kind signature

2026-10-28: NO LONGER REQUIRED (grader NO_LONGER_AN_OBLIGATION, skeptic NO_LONGER_AN_OBLIGATION). 2026-10-29: NO LONGER REQUIRED. 2026-10-30: N/A. Graded on all four transports: not applicable. Bead: -.

"The enum mirrors the branches in `product-format-declaration.json` and is closed for producer validation: sellers do not mint ad-hoc values. SDKs may retain unknown future values in tolerant in-memory parsers, but that does not change versioned JSON Schema validation. A seller responding to a 3.1-or-earlier request MUST project away 3.2-only kinds and emit a compatible legacy declaration; a strict older schema correctly rejects an unprojected newer kind." (and core/creative-asset.json: "format_kind": {"$ref": "/schemas/core/canonical-format-kind.json", ...})

static/schemas/source/core/canonical-format-kind.json#/description (plus #/enum, 16 values) at v3.2.7; static/schemas/source/core/creative-asset.json#/properties/format_kind. Since the previous audit: The spec text did not change: `git diff v3.2.1 v3.2.7 -- static/schemas/source/core/canonical-format-kind.json` is empty. The audit row's source was never the spec. It cited adcp-client-python PR #1415 and the `is_canonical_format_kind` docstring, which are SDK design notes. At both pins the spec says the opposite of the row: the format_kind vocabulary is the versioned spec's closed 16-value enum ("closed for producer validation"), and a strict versioned schema "correctly rejects" a newer kind. The code changed after the audit, so the audit's evidence is stale. The `supported_kinds` parameter of `creative_format_identity` (the audit's _base.py:689) was deleted on purpose (_base.py:904-920). The vocabulary is now fixed by the `CanonicalFormatKindStr` annotation on `CreativeFormatIdentity.kind` (_base.py:733).

Graded

Ungraded, and nothing grades it. No BDD scenario sends an out-of-vocabulary format_kind to sync_creatives or create_media_buy (searched tests/bdd for not_a_kind and out-of-enum kinds).

Evidence

This is not a spec obligation at v3.2.7. core/canonical-format-kind.json is a closed 16-value enum, 'closed for producer validation', and the row's source is an SDK docs PR (adcp-client-python #1415). The seller follows the pin: src/core/tools/creatives/_validation.py:150-156 refuses an out-of-enum kind with VALIDATION_ERROR on field format_kind, and FormatSelector.kind is CanonicalFormatKindStr (src/core/schemas/_base.py:685).

Gaps

No row is needed. The pin's real rule, refusing an out-of-enum format_kind, is graded by no BDD scenario on any transport, only by tests/unit/test_format_identity_kind_is_mandatory.py. If the owner wants it graded, the scenario must settle the code. Production answers VALIDATION_ERROR, but this repo answers out-of-enum schema violations elsewhere with INVALID_REQUEST (UC-011, UC-019). error-code.json defines VALIDATION_ERROR as 'beyond schema validation', so check whether the pin accepts VALIDATION_ERROR here before writing the Then.

The 2026-10-28 grade (tree 5bf5677fb, outcomes from innet_181026_1634)

Grader: NO_LONGER_AN_OBLIGATION. Skeptic: NO_LONGER_AN_OBLIGATION (agrees). Applies to this seller: yes (A sales agent receives `format_kind` on sync_creatives (core/creative-asset.json) and emits it on list_creatives and product format_options, so the question of which vocabulary is accepted does reach this seller. The RFC row's claim, that the accepted vocabulary belongs to the seller and the pin is no ceiling, has no source at v3.2.7. The pin binds the vocabulary to the version's closed enum. So the row grades nothing as a spec obligation. The pin's actual rule is to reject a value outside the 16 pinned kinds, and that rule does apply here.). Graded on all four transports: no. Bead: -.

At v3.2.7, V1 is not a spec obligation, and it never was one at the audit's pin: the canonical-format-kind.json text is identical at v3.2.1 and v3.2.7. The pin defines the format_kind vocabulary as the version's closed 16-value enum, "closed for producer validation". It permits SDKs to keep unknown values only in tolerant in-memory parsers, and says a strict versioned schema "correctly rejects" a newer kind. The row's claim that the seller owns the vocabulary and the pin is no ceiling comes from an adcp-client-python docs PR and the SDK docstring, not the spec. The seller now does what the pin says. The `supported_kinds` seam the audit cited was deleted. `CreativeFormatIdentity.kind` is `CanonicalFormatKindStr` (_base.py:733), and sync_creatives refuses an out-of-enum kind with VALIDATION_ERROR on field format_kind (_validation.py:141-148). No BDD scenario grades V1 or the pin-conformant refusal; only unit tests cover the type, so there are no baseline outcomes on any transport. The canonical_formats storyboard requires comply_test_controller, which this seller does not include, so it grades nothing here either.

Code

  • src/core/schemas/_base.py:73 Imports `CanonicalFormatKindStr` from adcp.types.canonical_creative. This is the SDK's opt-in annotation restricted to the pinned 16-kind vocabulary (an AfterValidator built by require_canonical_format_kind()).
  • src/core/schemas/_base.py:733 `CreativeFormatIdentity.kind: CanonicalFormatKindStr`: an identity with a kind outside the pinned enum cannot be constructed.
  • src/core/schemas/_base.py:904 Docstring of creative_format_identity (def at :878) records that the `supported_kinds` seam was REMOVED. The vocabulary is fixed at class-definition time, so a per-tenant or seller-owned set cannot be honoured. This is the audit's 'pin-as-ceiling', now chosen on purpose, and it matches the pin.
  • src/core/tools/creatives/_validation.py:32 `_CANONICAL_KIND = TypeAdapter(CanonicalFormatKindStr)`: the pinned-vocabulary validator on the sync path.
  • src/core/tools/creatives/_validation.py:141 sync_creatives refuses a `format_kind` outside the pinned 16 with AdCPValidationError(field="format_kind", details=ValidationDetails(rejected_value=...)). The comment states this is the only refusal on the sync path, because the DTO's format_kind is an open str.
  • src/core/format_resolver.py:286 On the canonical arm, creative_identity_of builds the identity from the declared kind. An out-of-vocabulary value is refused on construction by CreativeFormatIdentity.

Gaps

  • The obligation itself is not a spec obligation at v3.2.7. Its only source is an SDK docs PR (adcp-client-python #1415) and the docstring of adcp.types.canonical_creative.is_canonical_format_kind. The RFC/audit row should be restated against static/schemas/source/core/canonical-format-kind.json#/description ('closed for producer validation') or removed. Read as an obligation, it would ask the seller to accept kinds its versioned schema rejects.
  • No BDD scenario grades the pin's real rule: sync_creatives refusing a creative whose `format_kind` is outside the 16-value enum. grep over tests/bdd/features for out-of-vocabulary, unknown or invalid format_kind values on sync_creatives finds nothing. The refusal at src/core/tools/creatives/_validation.py:141-148 (VALIDATION_ERROR, field format_kind, rejected_value) is covered only by unit tests: tests/unit/test_format_identity_kind_is_mandatory.py:51 tests the type, not the wire. So it is not graded on a2a, mcp, rest or e2e_rest.
  • The audit row's evidence is stale. _base.py:689 `supported_kinds` and _base.py:714-726 no longer exist in that form. The seam was deleted (_base.py:904-920), and the vocabulary is now the `CanonicalFormatKindStr` annotation at _base.py:733 and _validation.py:32.
  • Adjacent obligations from the same pin description belong to other rows: projecting away 3.2-only kinds when a 3.1-or-earlier request is answered, and V2's per-product closed set (`format_options[]`). V1 should not count either one.
  • The pinned vocabulary is also enforced on the create_media_buy selector path. FormatSelector.kind is CanonicalFormatKindStr | None (src/core/schemas/_base.py:668), and PackageRequest.format_kind and Creative.format_kind are plain `str | None` on the DTOs. So an out-of-vocabulary package selector is refused when the FormatSelector is constructed, not by the DTO. No BDD scenario sends one there either, so this second path of the pin's real rule is also ungraded on every transport.
  • The canonical_formats storyboard is not the only one that requires the controller. At v3.2.7, protocols/creative/scenarios/canonical_supported_formats.yaml, universal/canonical-format-validate-input.yaml and universal/premium-display-canonical-validation.yaml all declare `requires: [controller]` too, so none of them grades anything for this seller.

V2 IMPLEMENTED Refuse a format_kind absent from the target product's format_options[] (the per-product closed set — a rule PR #1415 is at pains to say did not move to the seller, and is still enforced by validate_format_kind_in_options / FormatKindNotInClosedSetError ). core/product-format-declaration.json ; PR #1415

2026-10-28: IMPLEMENTED (grader IMPLEMENTED, skeptic IMPLEMENTED). 2026-10-29: IMPLEMENTED. 2026-10-30: IMPLEMENTED. Wave 8 (tree ef4d8f5fa): IMPLEMENTED. Graded on all four transports: partly. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.27.

**Closed-set semantics (normative).** `format_options[]` is the closed set of accepted formats for this product. Sellers MUST reject `create_media_buy` requests targeting any `format_kind` (or format option reference) not present in this list — typically with `UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE` or a seller-specific code; the rejection is structural, not negotiable. `seller_preference` modulates *within* the accepted set (a soft ranking hint between equally-acceptable options), it is NOT an enforcement axis.

static/schemas/source/core/product-format-declaration.json#/description § Closed-set semantics (normative) (adcp tag v3.2.7, 8d1d3244b). Since the previous audit: The obligation text is unchanged. The closed-set paragraph is byte-identical at v3.2.3 and v3.2.7. The audit's ABSENT verdict and its evidence are stale. `FormatKindNotInClosedSetError` is now imported at src/core/format_satisfaction.py:64 and raised in `_kind_route` (line 770). `git grep` for it in src/ no longer returns zero. The SDK's `validate_format_kind_in_options` is still not called. The membership test is done inline because the SDK helper is typed `Iterable[Format]` and the product holds the 16-arm `ProductFormatDeclaration` union. That is filed as salesagent-ol033.17 and changes no behavior.

The 2026-10-28 grade (tree 5bf5677fb, outcomes from innet_181026_1634)

Grader: IMPLEMENTED. Skeptic: IMPLEMENTED (agrees). Applies to this seller: yes (The rule binds a seller that receives create_media_buy against products declaring format_options[]. This sales agent receives create_media_buy and authors format_options[] on its products. It is not a creative-agent rule.). Graded on all four transports: yes. Bead: salesagent-ol033.20.27.

V2 is still an obligation at v3.2.7, word for word as at 3.2.3. Sellers MUST reject a create_media_buy that targets a format_kind or format option reference absent from the product's format_options[], typically with UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE. The audit's ABSENT verdict is out of date. Production now enforces the per-product closed set in src/core/format_satisfaction.py: `_kind_route` (line 770) raises the SDK's FormatKindNotInClosedSetError for a kind outside the product's declarations, and `_option_route` (line 696) refuses an unpublished option ref. create_media_buy runs this through `_resolve_package_selectors` (media_buy_create.py:753, called at :3174, before the manual-approval branch) and through the creative gate (:704). Both answer UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE via `canonical_refusal`. The same check also covers update_media_buy and sync_creatives assignment. The SDK's `validate_format_kind_in_options` is not called; the two-line membership test is inlined because of a type mismatch (salesagent-ol033.17), with no behavior difference. Grading comes from BDD, not the canonical_formats storyboard, which needs the controller this seller does not have. The refused-outline rows unpublished_video_kind, unpublished_option_ref and wrong_publisher_domain, plus the creative-refused scenario, assert UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE, the pinned recovery and the field on the wire envelope. Accept-outline controls stop them passing vacuously, and all of them passed on a2a, mcp, rest and e2e_rest in innet_181026_1634. Remaining gaps: no refusal row against a canonical-only product, no unpublished-kind row with manual approval, stale feature and docstring comments (the 'RED ON PURPOSE' block, and claims that the 3.2.3 storyboard grades this), and a baseline run at spec 3.2.3 rather than HEAD's 3.2.7.

Code

  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:770 _kind_route: `accepted` is the PRODUCT's declared kinds (line 769). When `kind not in accepted`, it builds FormatKindNotInClosedSetError(kind, accepted) and returns a KIND_NOT_PUBLISHED refusal carrying rejected_value and accepted_values. The set is per product, not the global 16-kind vocabulary.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:696 _option_route: a format_option_ref whose (publisher_domain, format_option_id) identity is not among the product's declarations returns OPTION_NOT_PUBLISHED. This is the '(or format option reference)' half of the rule.
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:804 _canonical_lane dispatches option -> legacy -> kind for a product that declares format_options[] (lines 804-808).
  • src/core/format_satisfaction.py:1206 canonical_refusal: one constructor for the wire refusal. It returns AdCPCapabilityNotSupportedError (UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE, src/core/exceptions.py:1389/1407) with CapabilityRefusalDetails{capability, rejected_value, accepted_values}.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:753 _resolve_package_selectors resolves every package's format_kind, format_option_refs and format_ids routes against the product's declarations. A non-legacy refusal is raised as canonical_refusal at line 828, with field packages[i].<arm>.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:3174 The selector gate runs before the manual-approval branch (line 3181), so the closed set holds whatever the tenant's approval mode, before any state is persisted.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:704 Creative-side create gate: a stored creative whose canonical kind is outside the product's closed set is raised as UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE, field=packages. The verdicts come from satisfies() at line 466.
  • src/core/tools/media_buy_update.py:509 The same refusal on the update_media_buy creative-assignment path, beyond the create-only pin text.
  • src/core/tools/creatives/_assignments.py:280 The same refusal on the sync_creatives assignment path. It is raised in both validation modes, so lenient mode does not soften it.

Gaps

  • Stale feature comment, tests/bdd/features/BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature:590-607. It says the selector gate runs after the manual-approval branch (':3337', 190 lines later) and that the approval scenarios are 'RED ON PURPOSE'. Production now resolves at src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:3174, before the branch at :3181, and @T-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION-approval-gate-refuses passed on all four transports in the baseline.
  • Grading claims that point at the storyboard. The feature header (BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature:10, :51) and src/core/format_satisfaction.py:28 say the rule is 'Graded by protocols/media-buy/scenarios/canonical_formats.yaml (3.2.3)'. That storyboard needs comply_test_controller, which this seller does not have, so it grades nothing here. The header also calls 3.2.3 'the pinned version'; the pin is 3.2.7.
  • No closed-set refusal against a canonical-only product. Every refused row seeds a dual-arm product (format_ids plus format_options). The canonical-only outline (BR-UC-FORMAT-SATISFACTION.feature:694) has accept rows only, so 'canonical-only product refuses an unpublished format_kind or option ref' is ungraded. That is the shape 3.2 tells new products to author.
  • No row combines manual approval with an unpublished kind or option ref. The closed set on the manual-approval path is graded only through the params-rule selector (bare_image_kind, feature:613).
  • Refusal Then steps (tests/bdd/steps/domain/uc_format_satisfaction.py:812-833, :1269-1280) assert code, pinned recovery and field on the wire envelope. They do not assert details.rejected_value or details.accepted_values (the product's kinds). The pin requires only the rejection, so this is not an obligation gap. It does mean the returned accepted set is ungraded.
  • Baseline vintage. innet_181026_1634 ran at commit 578c0079 with adcp 9.0.0b3 (spec 3.2.3). HEAD pins adcp 9.3.0 (spec 3.2.7). Since the baseline, the relevant src, steps, conftest and e2e_rest ledger are unchanged, and the feature changed only in @source comment lines. The obligation text is identical at both tags. Still, no run at HEAD's pin confirms the four-transport pass.
  • A fifth canonical_refusal raise site on the create path is not in the evidence: src/core/tools/media_buy_create.py:4259, in the package loop. It re-runs satisfies() over stored creatives against the product, marks the workflow step failed and raises UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE. Business behavior is consistent, but it duplicates the pre-validation at :704 (a DRY concern), and no scenario distinguishes which of the two sites answered.
  • The refusal Then steps also skip details.capability (FormatRefusal.KIND_NOT_PUBLISHED / OPTION_NOT_PUBLISHED). On the wire, the closed-set refusal of unpublished_video_kind therefore looks the same as a PARAMS_NOT_SATISFIED refusal with the same field. Today the code path decides which one fires, not the assertion. This sits alongside the colleague's point about rejected_value and accepted_values.
  • The sync-refused and sync-lenient Then steps (uc_format_satisfaction.py:1027) assert code and recovery only, with no field and no details.creative_id or package_id. That path's refusal is graded only by the code plus the accepted sibling scenario.
